# Changelog

## [Unreleased]

### Security
- **Full security audit: leaks & hacks — 12 findings fixed, 1 operator action required** (2026-09-13): audit over app leaks/attacks → fixes below. **C1 (rotation is operator action):** live plaintext secrets existed in `.env`/`server/.env` (PAYMENTO_API_KEY/SECRET, CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN, keystore passwords) and were already exposed once — **must be rotated**; `server/.env` held `JWT_SECRET=CHANGE_ME` → server now **fails hard on weak/seeded JWT secrets** (`length < 32` or member of a blocklist `{change_me,changeme,secret,password,admin,test,default,jwt_secret}` → exit with a `randomBytes(32)` hint, `server/signaling-server.ts`). **C2 (landing stored XSS closed):** `public/landing/app.js` built HTML from `releases.json`/`data/ticker.json`/i18n values via raw `innerHTML` — all 3 sinks now escape through a new `escapeHtml()` (i18n applyLang, `tickerHTML`, download-card builder incl. href/download attrs). **H1 (master seed at rest):** `src/lib/identity/masterKey.ts` stored the root seed as plaintext hex in IndexedDB; now wrapped AES-GCM under the device-bound key (fingerprint PBKDF2-600k, `deviceSecurity.getDeviceBoundKey()`), stored as `enc:v1:<iv>:<cipher>`; legacy plaintext auto-migrates in place; unwrap failure throws (identity preserved → recovery phrase restores) instead of silently regenerating; `RecoveryManager.restoreFromPhrase` bypasses `getMasterKeySet`, so seed recovery is unaffected; non-browser envdegrades to legacy with a warn. **H2 (WS register hardening):** `server/signaling-server.ts` `register` now validates `publicKey` strictly (length ≤128, `[A-Za-z0-9+/=_:.-]`) and **rejects a key already bound to a different live socket** (`ws.close(4001)` — never silently replaces the owner, defeating key hijack/impersonation); close-handler already only deletes `clients` when `clients.get(key)===ws`. **H3 (TOTP secret leak):** `server/cli.ts` logged the full `otpauth://…?secret=` URI on QR failure + good path; the failing path now prints a truncated `Secret: abcd…wxyz` hint, the full-URI `console.log` is removed. **H4 (verify token out of URL path):** `GET /api/paymento/verify/<token>` → `POST /api/paymento/verify` with `{token}` in the JSON body (token cap 512, rate-limit `paymento-verify:ip:token` 30/min, missing token → 400); client `verifyPayment` (src/services/paymento.ts) switched to POST; consumers `PaymentRequestCard`/`PaymentChatBubble` untouched (mocked). **M2:** TOTP window `1`→`0` (`server/auth.ts`; 90s→30s acceptance). **M3:** CAPTCHA CSPRNG — server `generateCaptchaChallenge` now uses `randomInt` from `node:crypto`; **admin SPA `Login.tsx` dropped its client-side `Math.random()` captcha and now fetches the server-issued challenge** (`api.login('','')` → `{needsCaptcha,sessionId,challenge}`, refresh re-fetches, submit sends `captchaSession`+`captchaAnswer`, server `error` classified, 403 CAPTCHA → localized error) — this also fixes the pre-existing broken admin login (server always required its own captcha the SPA never sent). **M4:** per-account brute-force limiter in `handleLogin` (`user:` + lowercased username, applied to known AND unknown usernames to prevent enumeration timing; 5/60s + 5-min lockout same as IP limiter). **M5:** merchant `api_key` no longer persisted raw per payment row — `server/routes/paymento.ts` stores a 16-hex `sha256(ENV_API_KEY)` fingerprint on `insertPayment` (both call sites). **M6 documented (accepted):** CSP `style-src 'unsafe-inline'` kept in `public/_headers` — React `style=` attributes and landing `el.style.*` require it; removing would break styling. **Verified non-issues:** SQL injection 0 (prepared stmts), command injection 0, eval/new Function/document.write 0, `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` 0, admin static path traversal guarded, JWT HS256 pinned, CORS/CSWSH allowlists, paymento IPN constant-time HMAC, bcrypt cost 12. D1–D5: no UI controls added except admin Login captcha refresh (live → `loadCaptcha`) — 0 findings. Regression: `src/lib/identity/masterKey.test.ts` +2 (encrypted roundtrip `enc:v1:` no-plaintext-leak + legacy-plaintext migration), `src/services/paymento.test.ts` re-target (POST body), `server/__tests__/paymento.test.ts` verify tests → POST + new missing-token 400; auth/auth-captcha tests green. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest (targeted green; full below), admin build clean (tsc+vite). **⚠ OPERATOR ACTION REQUIRED: rotate PAYMENTO_API_KEY, PAYMENTO_SECRET_KEY, CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN, BUBBLEWRAP_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD, BUBBLEWRAP_KEY_PASSWORD and set a strong (≥32 char random) JWT_SECRET on the server before restart.**
- **`vitest` bump to 4.1.11 (GHSA-82fw-gwwq-j7x9)** (2026-09-12): `npm audit` flagged a moderate path-traversal / arbitrary-file-read in `@vitest/mocker` (advisory range `2.1.0-beta.1 – 4.1.10`); lockfile pinned `4.1.9` despite `^4.1.8` spec. Bumped to `vitest@^4.1.11` (mocker resolves `4.1.11`); lockfile synced. Remaining 6 advisories (4 moderate, 2 high) are the documented dev-only `@bubblewrap/core` chain (`extract-zip`, `file-type`) — no fix available, not in the production bundle. `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high` → 0 vulnerabilities. e2e re-target: `settings.spec.ts` font-size assertion `[data-font-size]` → `html` (strict-mode violation after the 09-12 appearance fix mirrored the attr onto `documentElement`). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5758/5758 (332 files), e2e 196/196.

### Fixed
- **DM/P2P multi-photo album — N sequential FTR transfers + `abm1:` manifest first (feat, wire v2, reference `chat-ui_20_09.html` parity)** (2026-09-20): the DM/`Attach file` picker now accepts multiple images; the album bridges peer-to-peer as one bubble. **Wire** (`lib/fileTransfer/frames.ts`) — new `ALBUM_MAGIC = 'abm1:'` transport block: `AlbumEntry { transferId, name, mime, size }` + `AlbumManifest { albumId, messageId, chatId, chatName, senderName, timestamp, silent?, entries[] }`; `encodeAlbumManifest` / strict `parseAlbumManifest` (scalar+SafeInteger+size gates, `entries.length ≥ 2`, malformed → null). **Sender** (`useFileSend.sendFiles`) — validates a 10-file cap + per-file premium size/type gates; if 1 file delegates to `sendFile` (single path untouched); messages built with `type:'image'`, `msg.attachment = FTR_MAGIC + entries[0].transferId` (legacy consumers keep working) and a new `msg.album` array of `{url: FTR_MAGIC+transferId, fileName, fileSize}`; `appendMessage` once; **manifest is sent FIRST** (addressed raw payload via `safeSendRaw`) so the receiver renders the album bubble before bytes, then a sequential per-entry sha256→meta→chunk→end pipeline (same idb `saveTransferMeta`/`saveChunk` chain as single files); status `sent`/`queued` by `navigator.onLine`, failure → `failed` + `fileTransfer.sendFailed` toast, `sendingRef` released in `finally`. **Receiver** (`useP2PMessages`) — `ALBUM_MAGIC` dispatch branch (before FTR); new `handleAlbumManifest` validates dedupe by `albumId`, registers `albumTransferIds` (Set, cap 500 with `incomingAlbums`), binds peer + chat peer, appends ONE album bubble (send-time ordering, status `delivered`, silent passthrough) and acks `chat-ack`; subsequent `meta` frames for album transferIds still `saveTransferMeta` (bytes/download pipeline unaffected) but **skip** `appendIncomingToDmChat` — no duplicate single-image bubble. **Renderer** (`AttachmentMedia`) — new module-level `AlbumTile` subcomponent resolves per-tile `ftr1:` URLs through `useFtrBlobUrl` (Loader2 spinner while pending, non-ftr blob urls pass through), wired into the existing 2-col grid with lazy/`onError`→ImageOff/`+N` overlay unchanged. Regression: `frames.test.ts` +8 (round-trips, missing-prefix/malformed/rejected cases), `useFileSend.test.ts` +3 (2 files → manifest-first `ALBUM_MAGIC` raw, meta/chunk/end ×2 ordered, one append with `album[].length 2`; single-file delegate; 12 files → cap 10), `useP2PMessages.test.ts` +2 (manifest → album bubble + suppressed duplicate incoming, non-album payload leaves chat untouched). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5973/5973 (341 files), l10n PASS (0 errors).
- **Album multi-photo grid — channel posts with a 2-column preview + ``+N`` overlay (feat, reference `chat-ui_20_09.html` parity)** (2026-09-20): the channel owner composer now accepts multiple images/videos at once and renders them as an album (Telegram/reference image-grid). **ChatInputArea** — `pendingMedia` is now an array; the hidden `channel-post-media-input` gained `multiple` and slices up to 10 files per pick (`URL.createObjectURL` per file, `e.target.value=""` so repeat picks can stack); the preview row maps one thumbnail per item (each with a remove X); send gates/primary styling key off `pendingMedia.length`. **useChatPreviewState.sendMessage** — accepts single attachment or array; arrays build `newMessage.album = attachments.map(...)` while keeping `msg.attachment = album[0].url` (legacy consumers `ReplyQuote`/`ChatListItem`/media tabs keep working; single-image behavior byte-identical). **AttachmentMedia** — image branch renders a `grid-cols-2 gap-1` album grid (first 4 tiles, aspect-square `object-cover`, lazy, per-tile `onError` → `ImageOff` fallback, `autoLoadBlocked` → placeholder + Load button reusing the single-image pattern); the 4th tile gets a centered `bg-black/40` overlay with a big bold ``+N`` (remaining count) when the album has more items; tile click opens the existing photo viewer with the first item. Regression: `AttachmentMedia.test.tsx` +3 (album describe: 5-photo → 4 tiles + `+1`; 2-photo → plain 2 tiles, no overlay; grid click → viewer opened with first url), `ChatInputArea.test.tsx` +2 (multi-file attach + remove-thumb, album post sends array len 2) and the single-file post assertion updated to `arrayContaining`. DM/P2P multi-file (N sequential ftr transfers into one album message) = follow-up stage, needs wire-change approval. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5961/5961 (341 files), l10n PASS (0 errors).
- **Image bubble size pill — reference `chat-ui_20_09.html` parity (UX §2.2)** (2026-09-20): image bubble now overlays a `image-meta-row`-style dark pill (bottom-right, `rgba(0,0,0,0.45)` + `backdrop-blur`, `text-[11px]` white bold, `pointer-events-none`) showing the attachment size (`formatSize`) whenever `msg.fileSize` is known — same pattern as the existing video-duration pill; hidden when fileSize absent; click-to-open unchanged. Regression: `AttachmentMedia.test.tsx` +2 (pill shown with fileSize `200 KB`, hidden without; 13/13). Survey confirmed reference typing-bubble (3 bouncing dots) and file download-link already exist in app. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5956/5956 (341 files), l10n PASS (0 errors).
- **Voice waveform Telegram-style extras — speed pill, remaining countdown, unheard dot, click-to-seek, avatar tile (UX §2.2, reference `chat-ui_20_09.html`)** (2026-09-20): voice bubbles brought in line with the reference mock. **(1) Playback speed** — `useVoiceWaveformAudio.ts` added `speed` state + `speedRef`; `startPlaybackAt` sets `source.playbackRate` (guard: real AudioParam object → `.value`, plain mock source → direct property) and `changeSpeed` applies live on the active source; `VoiceWaveform` renders a ≥44px pill cycling `1×→1.5×→2×→1×` (`a11y.playbackSpeed`, new key ×8 locales), shown only when ready (hidden while loading/error). **(2) Remaining countdown** — the mid-playback label now shows time LEFT (`duration − elapsed`; at 50% of 0:12 → `0:06`), not elapsed. **(3) Unheard dot** — incoming bubble with `progress < 0.001` and not playing shows an 8px accent dot before the countdown (reference sub-row). **(4) Click-to-seek** — waveform canvas `onClick` seeks proportionally to the click X (`handleSeek((x-left)/width)`, `stopPropagation`, `cursor-pointer`; only when ready and not a live stream). **(5) Avatar tile** — `name?` prop (wired from `AttachmentMedia` as `msg.sender`); `VoiceWaveform` renders a leading `Avatar size="sm"` in the flex row, self-resolving `src` from the store (`userProfile.avatar` for own / `contactAvatars[name]` for incoming). Regression: `VoiceWaveform.test.tsx` play-button target tightened (`getAllByRole('button')[0]` — a second button now exists), remaining-label assertion preserved at `0:06`, mock `playbackRate` number-guard added; targeted 60/60, full suite green. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5954/5954 (341 files), l10n PASS (0 errors).
- **Voice messages delivered over P2P — recorded audio now reaches the peer and survives reload (feat)** (2026-09-20): closes the long-documented limitation («audio bytes are still not transferred over P2P») — a recorded voice note is now transmitted over the existing text wire and persisted on the receiver so it replays after reload. Sender (`useMessageActions.sendVoiceOverP2P`): loads the Blob from IndexedDB (`voice_blob_<id>`), guards size ≤ `VOICE_P2P_MAX_SIZE` (32 MB) and chunk count ≤ `VOICE_P2P_MAX_CHUNKS`, sha256-hashes, then sends `chat-audio-meta` → sequential `chat-audio-chunk` (46 080 B base64 each, under the 64 KB wire cap) → `chat-audio-end` addressively (`peerForChat ?? peerForChatName`) and marks `sent`. Receiver (`useP2PMessages`): module-level `incomingAudioMetas`/`incomingAudioChunks` maps; meta validates fail-closed (size/chunk bounds, `chunkSize*totalChunks ≥ size`) and registers peer + chat peer; chunks dropped out-of-range/duplicate; end reassembles, verifies sha256 (mismatch → discard), stores the Blob via `saveVoiceBlob(messageId, blob)`, inserts the audio message (`voiceId`, `duration` `"m:ss"`, status `delivered`) into the name/chatId-matched DM via send-time ordering, and best-effort acks `chat-ack`. Rendering chain already handles it (`useVoiceBlobUrl` → IDB blob). Offline queue flushes queued audio over P2P too (best-effort `markMessageSent` when the Blob is gone). Regression: +4 `useP2PMessages.test.ts` (happy-path reassembly + ack, sha256 mismatch → discard, oversized meta → fail-closed, end-before-chunk → discard), `useMessageActions.test.ts` updated (live-Blob offline queue, best-effort flush). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5952/5952 (341 files).
- **ui-audit: `inert` swipe-bucket overlap false positives restored (e2e-only, §2.2)** (2026-09-19): после a11y-фикса 09-17 (pass-log «aria-hidden focus-trap») закрытые свайп-бакеты `ChatListItem`/`ContactItem` несут `inert`, но `e2e/ui-audit.spec.ts` интерактивный фильтр исключал только `[aria-hidden="true"]` → закрытые бакеты (левая полоса ~176px + правая ~164px, на 320px геометрически пересекаются) считались и давали ложные overlap-находки (`[overlap] button "Start Call" x "Mute": 62%`, `contacts "Video call" x "Remove from favorites": 100%`) → падали 9 тестов (no horizontal overflow × все вьюпорты + audit: mobile/desktop/tablet + zoom). Фикс в фильтре: `&& !el.closest('[inert]')` — закрытые inert-бакеты исключены (неинтерактивны + невидимы), открытые (non-inert) по-прежнему аудируются. App-код не тронут (`inert` = корректная a11y, прецедент 09-07 `inFixedBottomBar`). Результат: ui-audit 14/14. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5942/5942 (341 файл), ui-audit 14/14.
- **Group chat file/image send overwrote the visible history (stale-closure clobber in `useFileSend`)** (2026-09-19): e2e `groups.spec.ts` «shared media tab shows image and link from group history» failed — the image bubble never rendered, profile showed «No media yet». Instrumented root cause: `useFileSend.appendMessage` appended the image to zustand `chats[]` (functional `setChats` — survived) and to the display chat via `onUpdateChat` (App's `setActiveChat`), but the broadcast `safeSend` throws with no peer, and `sendFile`'s `catch` → `updateMessageStatus(msgId,"failed")` re-called `onUpdateChat({ ...chat, history: ... })` built from the **stale closure `chat`** (the pre-image 1-item object) — clobbering the display `activeChat.history` back to text-only, so the UI and profile media list lost the image (the store kept it). Fix in 2 files: `useChatPreviewState.ts:62` now passes `setActiveChat: onUpdateChat` into `useFileSend` (App's `setActiveChat` accepts functional updaters), and `useFileSend.ts` `appendMessage`/`updateMessageStatus` switched to an `if (setActiveChat) { functional prev-based } else if (onUpdateChat) { closure-based }` shape so the display update merges onto the latest `prev` instead of a captured snapshot. Regression: `useFileSend.test.ts` offline test updated (`saveTransferMeta` now expected ×2 — meta + local-completed, matching the 09-16 local-persist product fix; completed snapshot asserted). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5942/5942 (341 files), e2e 207/207 (groups 11/11).
- **Унифицированный акцент — design-system зелёный вместо фиолетового/оранжевого (UX §2.4, дизайн-док 19.09.26)** (2026-09-19): по дизайн-доку (docs/MessAnger_Unified_Design_System_19_09_26.md, спека: `#4ede63` + accent2 `#10b981`, soft `rgba(78,222,99,0.12)`) акцентная палитра приложения приведена к green-family. Жалоба 09-13 «все кнопки фиолетовые» коренилась в рассинхроне: `messenger.css` (glass-слой, `--msg-*`) уже был зелёный, а app-токены ещё держали фиолетовый dark `#6f7fff` / оранжевый light `#ea580c`. **Токены** (`src/styles/tokens.css`): dark `--accent #6f7fff→#4ede63` (rgb `111,127,255→78,222,99`), `--accent2 #965dff→#10b981`, `--accent-soft rgba(78,222,99,0.12)`, производные (`--waveform-played-other`, `--waveform-unplayed-other` 0.45, `--button-primary-bg`, `--button-secondary-hover-bg` 0.15, `--button-secondary-border` 0.3, `--toggle-active-bg`, `--player-progress-orange`) → green; light `--accent #ea580c→#059669` (rgb 5,150,102), `--accent2 #d97706→#047857`, `--accent-soft rgba(5,150,102,0.12)`, производные → emerald. `--button-primary-text` dark → `#0d1017` (тёмные чернила на ярком зелёном, дом-паттерн как `messenger.css .icon-button.primary`; light остался `#ffffff` на `#047857` ≈4.9:1 AA). `src/index.css` хардкод-fallbacks: radial-gradient + `:focus-visible` + `::selection` `111,127,255`→`78,222,99`, `150,93,255`→`16,185,129`, `#6f7fff`→`#4ede63`. Собран весь фиолетовый в коде: `#6f7fff`/`#965dff`/rgb-fallbacks были только в tokens.css + index.css, компонентов не касалось. **Runtime-производные** (`src/hooks/useAppearanceEffects.ts` переписан, 55→~140 строк): при установленном `accentColor` теперь зеркалит и производные — `--accent2` (PAIRED_ACCENT2-карта: `#4ede63→#10b981`, `#3b82f6→#1d4ed8`, `#8b5cf6→#6d28d9`, `#ec4899→#be185d`, `#f59e0b→#b45309`, `#ef4444→#b91c1c`, `#14b8a6→#0f766e`, `#6366f1→#4338ca`, иначе darken 0.78) + `--accent2-rgb`, `--button-primary-bg`/`--toggle-active-bg`/`--player-progress-orange`/`--waveform-played-other` = акцент, `--button-primary-text` по relative luminance (WCAG, lum>0.4 → `#0d1017` иначе `#ffffff`); helpers hexToRgbChannels/rgbFromHex/toHex/relativeLuminance/darkenHex/textOnAccent. **Дефолт стора**: `settingsSlice.ts:257` `accentColor ?? '#10b981'→'#4ede63'` + `AppearanceSettings.tsx` swatch-лист и 3 fallback'а → `#4ede63` (рантайм-акцент совпал со спекой). `ChatListView.tsx:159` лёгкая ветка заголовка каналов `text-purple-600` → `text-[var(--accent2)]` (единственный хардкод-пурпур в компоненте). Регрессия: `useAppearanceEffects.test.tsx` расширен (+5 ассертов на производные; новый тест: дефолт `#4ede63` → `--accent2 #10b981` + `--accent-rgb 78,222,99` + `--button-primary-text #0d1017`); 4 целевых файла 41/41, полный 5942/5942. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5942/5942 (341 файл). D1–D5: 0 findings (все хендлеры не тронуты — CSS-токены + хук-зеркалирование).
- **Play pre-launch: browserhelper edge-to-edge deprecated API calls vendored out (release 8 / 1.0.7)** (2026-09-19): Google Play Console pre-launch report (release 7 / 1.0.6) flagged 3 items — (1) edge-to-edge may not apply to all users (Android 15 enables it by default for SDK 35; app uses `displayOverride:["edge-to-edge"]`), (2) **unsupported APIs**: `android.view.Window.setStatusBarColor` / `setNavigationBarColor` called from `com.google.androidbrowserhelper.trusted.LauncherActivity.onCreate` and `com.google.androidbrowserhelper.trusted.splashscreens.PwaWrapperSplashScreenStrategy.customizeStatusAndNavBarDuringSplashScreen`, (3) PiP recommendation (already enabled via `supportsPictureInPicture`). Root cause: the deprecated calls live in the 3rd-party `androidbrowserhelper` AAR — **2.7.3 is the latest release** (Google Maven metadata lastUpdated 2026-08-13) yet still ships them (byte-scan of the 2.7.3 AAR + dexdump of the built AAB confirmed `LauncherActivity`/`Utils`/`WebViewFallbackActivity`/`EdgeToEdgeController`/`PwaWrapperSplashScreenStrategy` invoke `Window.set*Color`). The upstream repo (GoogleChrome/android-browser-helper) main branch already reworked these 5 classes to the modern API (`WindowCompat.enableEdgeToEdge()` + `androidx.core.view.insets.ColorProtection`/`ProtectionLayout` for splash bars). **Fix**: vendored the 5 upstream-main classes into `scripts/android-vendor/browserhelper/src/main/java/com/google/androidbrowserhelper/trusted/**` (kept `androidbrowserhelper` maven 2.7.3 for its other classes) and new `vendorBrowserhelper()` in `scripts/build-android.mjs` (wired after `patchGradleDeps`): finds/downloads the pristine AAR → strips its 7 offending `.class` entries (LauncherActivity, Utils, WebViewFallbackActivity, WebViewFallbackActivity$1/$2, EdgeToEdgeController, PwaWrapperSplashScreenStrategy) via `jar` → writes `android/app/libs/androidbrowserhelper-2.7.3-e2e.aar` → copies the vendored sources into the generated module → rewrites the app dependency to `implementation files(…)` + explicit transitive deps (`androidx.annotation 1.9.1`, `androidx.core 1.17.0`, `androidx.appcompat 1.7.0`, `androidx.browser 1.10.0`, `guava 33.4.8-android`). Vendored deltas vs upstream: `Utils` stripped of the two deprecated methods + helpers (kept `convertDrawableToBitmap`); `WebViewFallbackActivity` replaced the `Window.set*Color`/`getStatusBarColor` blocks with `WindowCompat.enableEdgeToEdge()` + plain `Color.WHITE` extra-read; `LauncherActivity` inlined `configureIntentBuilder` (API absent from 2.7.3 metadata) with the upstream `CustomTabColorSchemeParams`/`CustomTabsIntent.COLOR_SCHEME_SYSTEM` build. Verified: `gradlew assembleRelease`+`bundleRelease` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (JDK 22, AGP 9.0.1); dexdump of the AAB — the only remaining `Window.set*Color` invokes sit inside `androidx.core.WindowCompat.enableEdgeToEdge` (the recommended API; Play did not flag them in the pre-fix build either); `configureIntentBuilder` absent; `aapt badging` → `versionCode 8 versionName 1.0.7`, compileSdk 36. Bidirectional build test OK, artifact generation unchanged (`app-release-signed.apk` 1.09 MB + `app-release-bundle.aab` 1.15 MB). Build-script + android module only — no app-code change (lint/tsc/vitest unaffected).
- **Mobile prod — two dead nav CTAs live (Bug A: Calls «View contacts», Bug B: CRM «Open Premium») + SW v68→v69** (2026-09-18): live audit on Android emulator (new hardened bundle) found 2 production navigation CTAs that did nothing on mobile. **Bug A** — Calls empty-state «View contacts»: `FeatureViews.tsx` routed via `onNavigate?.('contacts')`, но `AppMainContent` не передавал `onNavigate` on mobile → клик был no-op (приложение никуда не вело). Фикс: 4 call sites в `FeatureViews.tsx` (CallsHistory/ChatsMain/Calls/ChatProfileStack) теперь вызывают `setView('contacts'/'chats')` напрямую — `onNavigate` prop убран. **Bug B** — CRM CTA «Open Premium»: `AppChrome`-обертка pill статуса перехватывала tap → CTA не получал клик; пилюле добавлен `pointer-events-none` (+ `[@media(hover:hover)]:pointer-events-auto`), клики проходят на CTA. Регрессия: +2 `FeatureViews.test.tsx` (Bug A: клик «View contacts» из Calls → переключение на Contacts view; Bug B: клик «Open Premium» в CRM → открытие Premium subview), +1 `AppChrome.test.tsx` (header CTA тапается поверх pill). Live-verify на новом бандле (SW v69: Calls → «View contacts» → открылись контакты; CRM → «Open Premium» → открылся Premium: PREMIUM STATUS Inactive + PLANS 30д $5 / 90д $12). Hardened build: 105 obfuscated chunks, 597 files / 39.7 MB. D1–D5: 0 findings
- **Prod deploy — crash fix + connection pill live (2026-09-18)**: user reported the `.slice` prod crash again plus «на андроиде запускаю приложение — вообще нет связи». Root cause: prod was serving the stale `main-BUu6fIvd.js` (built 08:25, BEFORE the 18:38 crash fix `21174aa`) — the `.slice` TypeError on numeric legacy chat ids was still live; the Android TWA's stale SW cache served a pre-seed-fallback bundle, so the connection state never resolved. Signalling verified healthy (`wss://mess.cvr.name/ws` OPEN, `/api/auth/token` 200, `/health` 200). Deployed the verified working tree in 4 thematic commits over `21174aa`, rebuilt hardened (105 obfuscated chunks), `deploy-all.ps1 -SkipBuild …` (SW v67→v68 — first run used `powershell` 5.1 and died on `-Encoding utf8NoBOM`, rerun via `pwsh 7`), 597 files/39.7 MB verified, HTTPS 200. Live-verified via Playwright: pill `aria-label="Connection: Прямое"`, `wss://mess.cvr.name/ws` → OPEN, 0 console errors; `String(` coercions present (41 contexts) in the served `main-CBdWQLRr.js`. D1–D5: 0 findings.

- **Contact profile crash on numeric legacy chat ids — `.slice()` on non-string (D-class)** (2026-09-18): user-reported prod crash «ошибка» — `.slice()` invoked on a value that is neither a string nor an array, in the ContactProfile subtree (minified `d`/`n`). Root cause: `SafetyNumberModal` is **always mounted** by `ContactProfileModal.tsx` (line ~420, outside the `isOpen` branch), so its body expression `(theirPublicKey || contactId).slice(0, 16)` runs on every profile render even while closed. `contactId` came from `contact?.id`, and `contact.id` can be a **number** — `ChatListView.tsx:208` builds contacts as `{ id: profileContact?.id ?? chat.id }`, and legacy chat ids are numeric (chatSlice `toggleArchive(id: string|number)`; chatSlice.test.ts:20 `{ id: 1 }`). Numeric id + no pinned identity → `(undefined || 5).slice(0,16)` → TypeError. Fixed by string-coercion in 3 files: `SafetyNumberModal.tsx:57,59` → `String(myPeerId).slice(0, 16)` / `String(theirPublicKey || contactId || '').slice(0, 16)`; `ContactProfileModal.tsx:256` → `String(contact.name || '').charAt(0)` (latent charAt crash); `company/ContactList.tsx:97` → `String(contact.name || '').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()` (unguarded name.slice). Regression: +2 `SafetyNumberModal.test.tsx` (numeric `contactId={5}` and numeric `myPeerId={7}` render without throwing, theirId falls back to coerced id). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5927/5927 (339 files).

- **PWA install banner removed — console warning gone + mobile strip space freed** (2026-09-18): the `Banner not shown: beforeinstallpromptevent.preventDefault() called...` console line (previously annotated 09-17 as an "expected informational message") disappears along with the whole custom-install surface. Deleted: `components/status/InstallAppBanner.tsx`(+test), `hooks/useInstallPrompt.ts`(+test), `AppShell.tsx` usage+import, `STORAGE_KEYS.PWA_INSTALL_DISMISSED` (`constants/storage.ts`), and the `pwa.*` i18n block in all 8 locales + its `allTests` parity block. - **Voice messages Telegram-style: send date + metadata + live elapsed clock** (2026-09-18): follow-up to the 09-17 voice redesign — user wanted the send date and full metadata visible in chat so notes are easy to find, plus an ideal look. **(1) Real send-date tracking**: messages previously persisted only an `HH:MM` string and every `formatDateLabel` inference collapsed history to "Today"/"Yesterday" relative to the current clock. Every creation site now stamps an epoch `ts` (`useMessageActions`, `useFileSend`, `useChatPreviewState` workspace path — `Date.now()`; incoming P2P text — `frame.timestamp`, the sender's true send time — `useP2PMessages`), the date-separator builder uses it (`formatDateLabel(time, ts)`, new ts-aware branch in `chatUtils`), and `MessageTimestamp` prefixes the clock with `formatShortDate(ts)` — nothing for today, localized `chat.yesterday` for the previous day, `{day} {shortMonth}` for older (8 locales already had `chat.yesterday`). Long-form date separators across the history also now resolve against the stored epoch. **(2) Waveform polish**: the play control is a real `<button>` (was div `role=button`, `aria-disabled` → `disabled`), and the duration label becomes a **live elapsed clock** while playing (`m:ss`, `tabular-nums`) — the display loop that advances `progress` during playback was silently dropped in an earlier refactor (only the seek slider ever wrote progress), now restored as a dedicated rAF effect in `useVoiceWaveformAudio` (single driver; the redundant per-frame `updateProgress` call inside the canvas draw loop removed). Regression: new `MessageTimestamp.test.tsx` (today = clock only, Yesterday prefix, older short-date prefix, stealth fuzz, status icons), `VoiceWaveform.test.tsx` +1 (seek → label flips 0:12 → elapsed clock), updated disabled-assertions. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5932/5932 (341 files).
- **Serverless stance verified — no domain string rendered in the app UI** (2026-09-18, doc/verify): audited every UI surface for the domain `mess.cvr.name` or any origin URL — none is rendered anywhere at runtime (only test fixtures and the required TWA trusted-origin `host` field). The app keeps the origin-derived signalling fallback `wss://<location.host>/ws` (`src/config/signalling.ts`, no hard-coded server — any address is the page's own origin) and relay only when `VITE_RELAY_PROXY_URL` is configured; the browser/TWA address bar is OS chrome outside the app DOM and the previously-deployed strip referencing it — the visible "domain line" — is gone with the install-banner removal above. No secrets, no remote host, fully client-side. No code change (already compliant).
- **Voice Blob persisted before the `blob:` URL dies (Blob-first persistence + dead preview path removed)** (2026-09-18): continuation of the 09-17 voice fix — recorded notes were still saved via a fetch of the transient `blob:` object URL, which is dead by the time the fetch runs on reload, so persistence silently stored nothing and replay fell back to the unavailable state. `voiceStore.persistVoiceBlob(id, source: Blob | string)` replaces `persistVoiceBlobFromUrl`: a live `Blob` is stored directly (no fetch), a string source keeps the legacy fetch fallback; best-effort, never throws. `LiveVoiceRecorder.onSend` is now `(audioUrl, durationStr, blob)` — stop builds the `audio/webm` `Blob` and hands it up; the preview/re-record UI is removed (it only played the dead blob URL). Blob threaded through `sendVoiceMessage(audioUrl, durationStr, blob?)` → `useActiveChatWorkspace` → `ChatInputArea` → `useRefMessageActions` → `ActiveChatWorkspace` → `ChatPreviewLayer`. Regression: `voiceStore.test.ts` (Blob-first direct save + legacy-string fetch paths, empty/failed/throwing sources), `useMessageActions.test.ts` (persistVoiceBlob mock + blob assertion), `LiveVoiceRecorder.test.tsx` (deterministic `vi.spyOn(URL, 'createObjectURL')`, `onSend` blob assertion). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5958/5958 (342 files).
- **Voice notes played static noise instead of the recording (synthesized-audio fallback removed + voice blobs persisted)** (2026-09-17): user «Чтото звуковые шум только слышен». Root cause: `useVoiceWaveformAudio` **deliberately synthesized a white-noise buffer** (`Math.random()`-fed one-pole filter + sine envelope via `ctx.createBuffer`) in its `catch` when the URL fetch/decode failed and, likewise, when neither `audioUrl` nor `stream` was supplied — and set `isReady = true`, so the play control looked live and clicking it played literal static through the analyser. Recorded notes are stored as `blob:` object URLs (`LiveVoiceRecorder` → `URL.createObjectURL`), which die on reload and are meaningless for the peer, so any replayed note fell straight into the noise branch — exactly the reported symptom. Fixes: **(1)** synthesis deleted — a failed fetch/decode now sets a new `loadError` state with no buffer at all, so an unavailable source stays silent; **(2)** `VoiceWaveform` play control is interactive only when `isReady` (`role="button"`, `aria-disabled`, dimmed `cursor-default` otherwise) and a load failure renders a visible `chat.voiceUnavailable` hint in place of the seek slider, the key added to all 8 locales; **(3)** voice notes are now durable: new `src/lib/voiceStore.ts` (`saveVoiceBlob`/`getVoiceBlob`/`deleteVoiceBlob`/`persistVoiceBlobFromUrl`, key `voice_blob_<id>` over `lib/idb`, best-effort fetch → Blob), `useMessageActions.sendVoiceMessage` stamps `voiceId` on the message and persists the recorded Blob, and new `src/hooks/useVoiceBlobUrl.ts` rebuilds a fresh object URL from IndexedDB (revoking it on unmount, falling back to the transient URL while the async read is in flight) which `AttachmentMedia` now feeds to the waveform. Known limitation (unchanged): audio bytes are still not transferred over P2P, so a peer without the Blob sees the unavailable state rather than noise. Regression: new `src/lib/voiceStore.test.ts` (6 — roundtrip, non-blob guard, delete, fetch persist, failed/empty fetch, thrown fetch), new `src/hooks/useVoiceBlobUrl.test.ts` (4 — IDB blob wins, transient fallback, no `voiceId` skips IDB, revoke on unmount), `src/components/chat-preview/VoiceWaveform.test.tsx` +2/−1 (no synthetic buffer without a source; decode failure → unavailable hint + `aria-disabled`, `createBuffer` never called), `src/hooks/useMessageActions.test.ts` voice test extended (`voiceId` on the message + `persistVoiceBlobFromUrl` call). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5959/5959 (342 files).
- **Chat attachments redesigned — voice/photo/video/file rendering now adaptive and type-aware (UX §2.1/§2.2)** (2026-09-17): user «доработай вид в чате голосовых сообщений, фото, видео, документов и т.п. чтобы вид максимально удобный и адаптивный был». Four fixes: **(1) documents** — every non-media bubble rendered the same generic `FileText` tile, so a spreadsheet, a PDF and a `.zip` were indistinguishable: new dependency-free `src/utils/fileType.ts` (`getFileExtension`, `getFileKind(fileName?, mime?)` → `pdf|image|audio|video|archive|doc|sheet|code|other`, extension map first with a MIME-prefix fallback) and `AttachmentMedia` now maps each kind to a tinted icon tile (`FILE_KIND_STYLE`: pdf rose, doc sky, sheet emerald, image violet, audio amber, video fuchsia, archive orange, code cyan, other slate). **(2) photos** — the image was `object-cover` at fixed max-heights (cropping portraits/landscapes), sat inside a fragment with a redundant `PHOTO`/`READY` chip floating next to the bubble, and had no width cap on desktop: now `object-contain` with natural aspect ratio, responsive caps (`max-w-[320px] sm:max-w-[360px] md:max-w-[420px]`, `max-h-[240px] sm:max-h-[300px] md:max-h-[360px]`), rounded on `var(--message-radius)`, and the chip is gone. **(3) video** — thumbnail was a fixed 180×100/200×120 crop with a 40px play circle: now a proper 16:9 `aspect-video` frame (`w-[240px] sm:w-[300px] md:w-[360px] max-w-full`), centred 48px glass play button with hover scale, and the duration badge promoted to `text-[11px] font-semibold` on `bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md`; the inline ftr player is width-capped the same way. **(4) voice** — waveform width was a fixed `w-[160px] sm:w-[220px]` regardless of length and the unplayed bars were hard-coded white (`rgba(255,255,255,0.3)`/`0.1`), i.e. near-invisible in light theme: the container now sizes by duration bucket (`<15s` 170px, `<45s` 220px, `<120s` 280px, else 320px, `max-w-full`; live streams stay full width) and the unplayed colour comes from the new `--waveform-unplayed-self` / `--waveform-unplayed-other` tokens (dark `rgba(240,242,245,0.35)` / `rgba(111,127,255,0.45)`, light `rgba(15,23,42,0.3)` / `rgba(234,88,12,0.35)`); duration text is now theme-aware for bubbles from others. - **Status pill claimed `🔁 Релей` for a direct connection (relay backend gated on a relay proxy)** (2026-09-17): the pill renders `relayed = transportBackend !== 'direct'` (`AppChrome.tsx:14`), and `transportBackend` is the persisted `relayBackend` setting (`connectionSlice` default `'direct'`, cycled in Settings → Network). Nothing tied that selection to whether the build actually has a relay endpoint, so a stale non-`direct` value (persisted from earlier sessions) put `🔁 Релей` on the pill while the socket still dialled the signalling origin straight — only `cfworker` rewrites the URL (`wsTunnel.ts`), `domainfront`/`peertunnel` open the same `wss://<origin>/ws`. Fix (house style = hide, not disable): new `resolveRelayProxyUrl(env)` + `RELAY_PROXY_URL`/`IS_RELAY_PROXY_CONFIGURED` in `src/config/signalling.ts` (env `VITE_RELAY_PROXY_URL`, set by `deploy-all.ps1` phase 0 next to the relay seed); `useAppConnection` honours the persisted backend only when a relay proxy is configured, otherwise forces `direct`; `NetworkSection` hides the relay-backend row entirely without one. Defensive companion fix: `WsTunnel.formatRelayUrl` accepted only bare hosts (`^https?://`), so the new absolute `wss://…` seeds would have produced a malformed `wss://wss://…` URL under `cfworker` — now accepts absolute `ws(s)://` too. `.env.example` documents both `VITE_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS` and `VITE_RELAY_PROXY_URL`. Regression: +3 `signalling.test.ts` (relay proxy trim/unset), +1 `useAppConnection.test.tsx` (new, 2 tests: persisted `cfworker` downgraded without proxy, honoured with proxy), +1 `NetworkSection.test.tsx` (row hidden), +1 `wsTunnel.test.ts` (absolute wss seed). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5938/5938 (339 files).
- **Connection pill stuck `○ Не в сети` on production (signalling seed fallback)** (2026-09-17): prod `mess.cvr.name` showed `transport.offline` while the UI rendered from IDB. Root cause: commit `c1d6d3a` (2026-09-16) removed `DEFAULT_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS` so `SIGNALING_SEED_URLS` became `[]` unless `VITE_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS` is set at build time; the deployed artifact is the **obfuscated** build (`build:hardened`, `scripts/build-web.ps1`), which — unlike `deploy-all.ps1:119` — never sets that env, so no seed URL was baked (verified: live `main-B2dMLsgr.js` has 0 occurrences of `cvr.name`/`workers.dev`/`/ws` in plaintext or base64). With an empty list `useAppConnection` force-set `disconnected`. Fix: `src/config/signalling.ts` gained a pure, testable `resolveSeedUrls(env, location)` — explicit `wss://` env seeds still win; otherwise it falls back to the **page origin** (`wss://<location.host>/ws` on https, `ws://…/ws` on http), which every standard deployment terminates at nginx (`location = /ws`); non-DOM/no-origin (SSR/Node) stays empty = serverless. No hard-coded host reintroduced (keeps the 2026-09-15 privacy stance). `useAppConnection` comment updated. Regression: new `src/config/signalling.test.ts` (6) — env override + invalid-entry filtering, same-origin https/http fallback, whitespace env treated unset, null origin and non-http(s) protocol → empty. - **Embed widget now self-contained + chat presence wired (embed CSS + online status)** (2026-09-17): «доработай недочеты, и посмотри почему не всети статус». (1) **Embed rendering on third-party pages**: `dist/embed.js` shipped 0 CSS and its JSX used Tailwind utility classes (`min-w-11`, `bg-[var(--accent)]`…) + theme variables that exist only under `html[data-theme]` in the app's `tokens.css` — on a cold site the widget was unstyled except inline accent fills. Rebuilt on pure semantic self-contained classes: `EmbedWidget.tsx` JSX → `.ew-root/.ew-panel/.ew-header/.ew-close/.ew-contact/.ew-field/.ew-btn-primary/.ew-btn-ghost/.ew-messages/.ew-msg/.ew-composer/.ew-input/.ew-send/.ew-fab/*` (+ `.ew-bot-left/.ew-bot-right` positioning, `data-ew-theme`/`data-ew-pos` attributes) and all styling moved to a new `src/embed.css` (light + dark palettes, local `--ew-*` fallback defaults so it renders without app tokens). `embed-entry.tsx` now imports the CSS as `?inline` and injects a one-shot `<style id="messanger-embed-style">` on boot (host-page-safe — no external asset load, immune to the host's CSP blocking a stylesheet link), and detects `prefers-color-scheme: dark` → passes `theme` to `mountEmbedWidget` (optional 3rd arg; default `'light'`). Bundled CSS + runtime `window.matchMedia` verified present in `dist/embed.js` (7808 B → 10820 B). (2) **Online status (root cause)**: `chat.online` (chatSlice factory = `false`) had ZERO live writers — the chat-list `.avatar-status` green dot, `ChatHeader` online pill, and `ChatProfileView` online/last-seen all read it and could never go green. The P2P layer already tracked authoritative connect/disconnect (`onConnected`/`onDisconnected` in `p2p/network.ts`) and the in-protocol `online-status` metadata signal (P2PTransport) was received but discarded. Wired it: `network.ts` — `transport.onMetadataSignal` gained an `else if (type === 'online-status')` branch (anti-replay passes through) that parses `{online}` via new `safeParsePresence` and notifies new `presenceObservers`; new `sendPresenceSignal(online)` broadcasts `online-status` over all live transports (paired with the typing-indicator pattern) and new `onPresence(cb)` subscription. New `src/hooks/useChatPresence.ts` (mounted once in `App.tsx` next to `useP2PBoot`): subscribes `onConnection` (→`sendPresenceSignal(true)` + mark chat online), `onDisconnection` (→ mark offline + `lastSeen`), `onPresence` (→ mark), takes an initial snapshot of already-connected peers on mount; `markChatsForPeer` maps peerId → chats via `p2pNetwork.peerForChat(chat.id) ?? peerForChatName(chat.name)` and calls `setChats` functional-update (no-op write skipped when unchanged). Both peers run the same code → symmetric status without relay presence. Contacts `online` field + privacy gating left as documented future work. Regression: +6 `useChatPresence.test.tsx` (new), `EmbedWidget.test.tsx` touch-zone selector `min-h-11` → `ew-send`. - **Swipe-action buckets: `aria-hidden` → `inert` (a11y focus-trap fix)** (2026-09-17): console reported `Blocked aria-hidden on an element because its descendant retained focus` — the chat-list and contact-list swipe overlays (`ChatListItem.tsx`, `ContactItem.tsx`) marked their closed swipe-action buckets `aria-hidden={true}` while the mute/archive/delete/call/video buttons inside remained focusable (clicking a swipe button or tabbing into the invisible bucket left focus inside an `aria-hidden` subtree; `aria-hidden` does not affect tab order, so keyboard users could also tab into invisible buttons). Per the WAI-ARIA spec guidance in the warning, both buckets now use the `inert` attribute instead (`inert={swipedOpen !== "left|right"}`) — React 19 renders it as a real attribute; closed buckets are now unfocusable AND removed from the a11y tree, open buckets behave as before. Regression: +1 `ChatListItem.test.tsx`, +1 `ContactItem.test.tsx` (2 closed buckets carry `inert` and no `aria-hidden`). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5909/5909 (336 files). Note: the `Banner not shown: beforeinstallpromptevent.preventDefault() called...` console line is Chrome's expected informational message for the custom install-banner pattern (`useInstallPrompt` captures + `InstallAppBanner` calls `deferred.prompt()`); it fires unconditionally when `preventDefault()` is used and cannot be suppressed while keeping the custom prompt — no code change.

- **TextInputModal value-clobber race killed (flake `LoginScreen` "imports encrypted backup")** (2026-09-16): the deploy-gate caught the known intermittent failure again (`decryptBackupFile` never called, modal silently closed — 09-15 CHANGELOG noted it as a one-off flake). Root cause finally identified: `TextInputModal` reset its value via a passive `useEffect [isOpen, initial]` → `setValue(initial)`. Under full-suite load (~334 worker files) the effect task can be scheduled AFTER the user/test types — the typed value gets clobbered to `""`, so `LoginScreen.confirmBackupPassword("")` early-returns after closing the modal: no decrypt call, no error, UI back at "Restore your data". Fix in `settings/TextInputModal.tsx`: the reset now happens synchronously in the render phase on the closed→open (or `initial`-change) transition (`prevOpen`/`prevInitial` refs), so typing can never slip ahead of it; the focus timeout stays in the now-single-dep effect. - **Device-bound wrapping key made non-extractable (OS secure storage)** (2026-09-15):`deviceSecurity.importRawKey` imports the fingerprint-derived AES-GCM wrapping key with `extractable: false` — the raw key material can no longer leave via `crypto.subtle.exportKey` (WebCrypto's closest analog to Keychain/Keystore; on OS-backed platforms this can route through the native key store). The master key stays extractable by design — `CryptoKey` is not structured-cloneable, so its raw bytes must be serialized and stored wrapped at rest by the device-bound key (overwrap already H1/P6). Regression: +1 `deviceSecurity.test.ts` (AES-GCM import now asserts `extractable: false`). Gates: targeted 4 files × 35 tests green; lint 0, tsc clean, full vitest run pending in receipt.
- **Late-arriving P2P frames land in send-time order (message ordering)** (2026-09-15): closed the roadmap «проверка порядка и пропусков» item — the ordered-reliable SCTP data channel (`createDataChannel(..., { ordered: true })`) makes sequence gaps structurally impossible and the timeout-based ACK pipeline already retransmits missing messages, so the real reliability bug was a *late retransmit arriving after newer messages*: `useP2PMessages` appended incoming chat-text frames in arrival order, so an ACK-pipeline re-send rendered below newer messages. Ingress now inserts by numeric id (`messageId` = sender's `Date.now()`, chronologically comparable on both sides) via `insertBySendTime`, so retransmitted frames land in send-time order. Regression: +1 `useP2PMessages.test.ts` (late frame '100' after '200' inserts before it). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5932/5932 (343 files).

- **Speaker toggle now routes audio (call UX)** (2026-09-15): closed the call-UX roadmap item (bluetooth/speaker/earpiece) — `toggleSpeaker` flipped the `isSpeaker` flag while the apply-sink effect only ran `setSinkId('default')` on ON (a no-op: desktop default is already the loudspeaker, mobile default is the earphone, so the toggle produced no audible change). `CallScreen` sink effect is now symmetric: OFF → `setSinkId('default')` (earphone/receiver), ON → `enumerateDevices()` → first `audiooutput` whose label matches `/speaker|external|loudspeaker|hands ?free/i` → `setSinkId(deviceId)`, falling back to `default` when labels are hidden or enumeration is unavailable. Regression: +3 `CallScreen.test.tsx` (off → `default`, on → speaker device by label, on with unlabelled devices → `default` fallback). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5930/5930 (343 files).
- **Read receipts suppressed on hidden tabs (overflow 3 privacy)** (2026-09-15): closed the roadmap item «не отправлять read receipt при preview, background или неактивной вкладке». `useChatPreviewState` now tracks `document.visibilityState` (init + `visibilitychange` listener) and the read-receipt effect skips while the tab is hidden — `lastReadReceiptRef` is not advanced, so the receipt is sent once the tab becomes visible again (chat marked read on return, standard behavior). ACK/typing unchanged: ACK is the sent→delivered protocol mechanism (`deliveryReceipts` setting is display-only), typing already gated by `typingIndicators`. Regression: +3 `useChatPreviewState.test.ts` (receipt on visible mount, none while hidden, sent after `visibilitychange` back to visible). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5927/5927 (343 files).
- **Storage exhaustion hardening (overflow 3)** (2026-09-15): closed the roadmap storage-exhaustion item — the IDB file-transfer store and the offline queue are now hard-capped so unbounded growth is impossible. `fileStore.ts`: `FILE_TRANSFER_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES` (256 MB), `FILE_TRANSFER_COMPLETED_RETENTION_MS` (24 h), `MAX_CONCURRENT_INCOMING_TRANSFERS` (4); new `pruneCompletedTransfers` (GCs completed transfers older than the retention window), `canAcceptFileTransfer(size)` (pre-acceptance byte-budget check, fails open on scan errors so a store issue never blocks a legitimate transfer), `enforceFileTransferBudget` (evicts completed transfers first — oldest `receivedAt` — then oldest incomplete). `useP2PMessages`: mount GC chain now runs `pruneAbandonedTransfers` → `pruneCompletedTransfers` → `enforceFileTransferBudget`; an incoming `meta` frame is rejected when ≥4 transfers are in flight (incomplete) or when the byte budget would be exceeded — no meta saved, chunks dropped, no DM bubble. `messageQueue.ts`: `MAX_QUEUE_ITEMS = 100` — `queueMessage` evicts the oldest unsent item before adding a new one; sent items are never evicted. Barrel export in `lib/index.ts`. Regression: fileStore +4, messageQueue +2 (cap eviction keeps sent items), useP2PMessages +3 (mount GC chain, in-flight rejection, budget rejection). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, targeted run 43/43 (3 files); full suite 5923/5924 (345 files) — 1 pre-existing flake `LoginScreen.test.tsx` “imports encrypted backup after entering password” (passes in isolation 19/19, outside this diff).
- **Release manifest XSS hardening** (2026-09-15): the downloads page now HTML-escapes all manifest fields, limits download targets to same-origin or HTTPS URLs, and binds checksum copying through an event listener rather than interpolating it in an inline handler. Malformed manifests degrade safely instead of breaking the page.
- **No hard-coded public messenger host** (2026-09-15): removed the default `mess.cvr.name` signalling endpoint and all built-in public invite/share hosts. Group, channel, CRM, and story shares now use `nexus://` identity links; relay use is opt-in through `VITE_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS`, and an unconfigured client remains disconnected instead of contacting a preselected server. The embeddable widget likewise has no default remote URL (`VITE_EMBED_WIDGET_URL` is required for self-hosted use).
- **Offline queue ACK pipeline: retry, backoff, max attempts, prune (overflow 3)** (2026-09-15): closed the roadmap ACK/offline-queue hardening item. `messageQueue` now supports `removeQueuedMessage(id)`, `pruneExpiredQueuedMessages(maxAgeMs = QUEUE_MAX_AGE_MS 7d)`, and `MAX_QUEUE_RETRIES = 5`. `useMessageActions` flush loop prunes expired queued items, retries failed text-capable items with `min(60_000, 1_000 * 2 ** retryCount)` backoff, evicts after 5 failed attempts and marks the chat message `failed`, continues processing later queue items, and retries on a 30s interval while online. `handleSendMessage` no longer swallows direct-send failures; failed direct P2P sends fall back to `queued`. `useChatPreviewState` no longer simulates `delivered`/auto-read for real P2P chats; status transitions only come from wire `chat-ack`/`chat-read`. Real-peer gate: `p2pNetwork.peerForChat(String(chat.id)) !== undefined` or `/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/`. Regression: messageQueue remove/prune tests, useMessageActions retry/eviction tests, useChatPreviewState wire-gate tests. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5923/5923 (345 files).
- **Offline flush text-capable fix (overflow 1)** (2026-09-15): `useMessageActions` flush used `if (item.data?.type !== undefined) continue;`, skipping every typed queue item. Now `const isTextCapable = item.data?.type === undefined || item.data?.type === "sticker"; if (!isTextCapable) continue;` — text/sticker flush over P2P, media remains queued because blob URLs are local-only. Regression: flushes queued text and sticker over P2P on reconnect, keeps media queued. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5923/5923 (345 files).
- **Incoming file integrity + transfer GC (overflow 2)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap overflow item «проверка файлов при приёме» is closed. `useP2PMessages` 'end' handler no longer marks transfers completed blindly — the blob is reassembled (`getTransferBlob`) and the declared `sha256` recomputed (`sha256Hex`, case-insensitive compare): mismatch → `completed: false, integrityError: true` (new `StoredTransfer.integrityError`), progress 0; match → completed. Chunk receive hardened: out-of-range chunks (unknown transfer / `index >= totalChunks`) dropped, duplicate indices deduped via per-transfer `Set` (exact progress, no double-save); `receivedChunks` + `receivedAt` now persist per chunk (was start/end only). New `pruneAbandonedTransfers(maxAgeMs = TRANSFER_ABANDON_TIMEOUT_MS 30 min)` in `fileStore.ts` GCs incomplete transfers silent > threshold, called on `useP2PMessages` mount; completed transfers kept (blob assembly outlives frames). Regression: fileStore +1 (prune deletes stale-incomplete, keeps recent/completed, chunks cleared), useP2PMessages +2 (bounds+dedupe drops; integrity mismatch flagged). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5915/5915 (345 files).
- **P2P security test suite (P8)** (2026-09-15): new `securitySuite.test.ts` — 6 security vectors on a real two-peer transport (real Ed25519 identity, real ECDH + HMAC, relay-wired signaling mock): tampered HMAC envelope dropped (`Invalid HMAC signature` warn, no delivery); replayed + out-of-order envelopes dropped (monotonic per-peer sequence, `incomingSequence`); oversized messenger frame rejected — NEW 64 KiB payload guard in `P2PTransport.send()` (`MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 65536`, exceeds → throw before envelope build); strict `chatFrame` parsers fail-closed (bad magic / negative seq / non-string ids / garbage JSON → null); TOFU pin rotation rejected (`verifyOrPinPeer` pin mismatch blocks key rotation, invalid signature blocked); wire `chat-ack`/`chat-read` roundtrip intact over a real two-peer channel (encode → relay → decode, byte-identical). Browser dual-peer e2e remains a documented limitation (needs per-context master-identity provisioning + signaling server in the e2e env). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5911/5911 (345 files).
- **Production peer discovery + mesh routing (P7)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap items «рабочее peer discovery» + «reconnect и peer lifecycle» are closed — mesh discovery now works end to end. `broadcastRaw` is a real fan-out to every connected direct peer (was an empty stub, so router adverts never left the node); DHT new-peer notification fixed (was reversed — `onNewPeer` fired only for pre-existing nodes, so new peers were never auto-connected); `onNewPeer` gained a self-dial guard + in-flight dial dedupe (reentrancy fix — `connectToPeer → MeshDHT.addNode(new) → notify → connectToPeer(same)` hung on recursion); router frames (`mesh-route-advert` / `mesh-forward`) are consumed by the router in `transport.onMessage` and never delivered as chat messages; multi-hop forward callbacks survive router recreation (`rebindRouterForward` in `init()`); TURN already wired into ICE servers with relay-only + backoff present. Regression: +4 `network.test.ts` (fan-out envelope, advert consumed by router, forward delivered for self via `onForward`, never-dial-self) +1 `MeshDHT.test.ts` (notify on new only). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5905/5905 (344 files — incl. P6 flake fix: async encrypt awaits `vi.waitFor` instead of fixed timeout).
- **Atomic encrypted persistence for TOTP/TURN (P6)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap item «атомарное сохранение TOTP и TURN credentials» is closed — secrets are now encrypted BEFORE their first write and never touch plaintext storage. `persistSetting` and `updateSettings` (`settingsSlice.ts`) removed the plaintext-first write: the encrypted branch encrypts first (AES-GCM), fails closed when the session key is missing (nothing persisted, save aborted mid-batch), and only then writes the `ENC:v1:` bundle; new `persistEncryptedSetting(key, value)` helper backs the atomic path. `syncSlice.setTotpSecret` now persists through that helper — previously it was state-only and TOTP was lost on reload. Regression: +1 `settingsSlice.test.ts` (no-key → nothing written; with key → only `ENC:v1:` stored, decrypt round-trip, hydration restore). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5900/5900 (343 files).
- **Strict frames + anti-replay on all channels (P5)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap items «защита от legacy frames» + «anti-replay для всех типов данных» are closed — every frame family now carries a mandatory payload sequence. Chat/ACK/read frames (`chatFrame.ts`): `seq` + required `messageId`, parsers reject legacy (no-seq) and malformed frames → `null`; file frames (`fileTransfer/frames.ts`): `seq` on meta/chunk/end + strict per-type validation (transferId, chunk index ≥ 0, non-empty data, positive sizes/chunks). Call-control data channel is now fully enveloped like the messenger channel (seq|sender|payload + HMAC + AES-GCM; legacy bare-JSON refused with a strict-mode warning) and the messenger receive path fixed: the monotonic seq counter is only advanced AFTER sender-key validation so a spoofed sender can no longer burn the sequence. Metadata signaling carries `seq` (server rejects `Invalid metadata seq`), per-type anti-replay dedup on receive, and typing payloads reduced to `{isTyping}` — display name resolved receiver-side (privacy: roadmap «не передавать display name»). Regression: +2 `P2PTransport.test.ts` (call-control envelope rejections, stale metadata dedup), +NEW `chatFrame.test.ts` (12), +2 `frames.test.ts`; fixtures across `useP2PMessages`/`useFileSend`/`frames` gained seq. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5899/5899 (343 files).
- **Mandatory peer identity verification: unsigned handshakes refused (P4)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap item «обязательная криптографическая identity-проверка» is closed — a handshake without a signed ephemeral DH key is now impossible. `call()`/`createPairingOffer()`/`pairingIdentityFields()` throw when local identity keys are missing; `handleOffer`/`handleAnswer` fail closed on offers/answers without `identityPub`+`dhSig` (warn + full session rollback) and run TOFU `verifyOrPinPeer` unconditionally in both directions; pairing `authenticatePairing` refuses unsigned payloads of any role; renegotiation answers and `onnegotiationneeded` offers always carry the Ed25519 signature. Regression: +3 relay `P2PTransport.test.ts` (offer/answer without signature fail closed + cleanup, `call()` throws without keys) + +2 pairing tests (`createPairingOffer` throws, stripped-signature offer rejected); pairing harness gains per-label stable identities (TOFU pins first identity per peer id). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5885/5885 (342 files).
- **Addressed P2P delivery: peer-confirmed ACK/read, per-chat routing (P3)** (2026-09-15): the roadmap item «адресная доставка» is closed — `p2pNetwork` now learns a chat↔peer binding from inbound frames (`rememberChatPeer(chatId, chatName, peerId)`, `peerForChat`/`peerForChatName` getters) and sends through the new `sendAddressed(target, data)`: addressed `sendTo` when the peer transport is live, `broadcast` fallback otherwise (returns `false`). All outbound chat paths rewired from blind broadcast: chat text (`useMessageActions`), preview chat text + read receipts (`useChatPreviewState`), file frames (`useFileSend` `safeSend`), and delivery ACKs (`useP2PMessages` — sender always known, always addressed). Inbound chat-text additionally binds the peer id to the chat/name maps. Regression: +1 `useP2PMessages.test.ts` (ACK addressed to the sender + peer bound to chat), mocks in 4 hook specs delegate `sendAddressed` → `broadcast` so old broadcast assertions stay green. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5880/5880 (342 files).
- **Real P2P calls: CallManager ↔ WebRTC transport (P2)** (2026-09-15): calls no longer purely simulated when the peer id is a real identity key. `CallManager` now routes through `p2pNetwork`: `startCall`/`acceptCall` (hex ed25519 peer ids only) attach media handlers, add the local stream and send `call1:` signaling frames over the authenticated data channel (`call-ring` / `call-accept` / `call-end`, new `CALL_MAGIC` + `encodeCallSignal`/`parseCallSignal` in `chatFrame.ts`); inbound ring frames wake `startIncomingCall`, remote tracks land on `activeCall.remotePeer.stream`/participants and flip `connecting→connected` (recording auto-start preserved), peer names cached via `rememberPeer`. `network.ts` Kadabra stub replaced — transports now use real signaling seeds, `peerId` = Ed25519 public hex from master key, new `isReady`/`getTransport`/`sendTo`/`isConnected`/`rememberPeer`/`getPeerName`; `P2PTransport` gained `lastDhPubHex` + `onnegotiationneeded` renegotiation (offer/answer reuse live pc, no key re-derive). Preview/name/uuid paths stay simulated (fallback contract keeps tests green), 64-hex guard only. Regression: +1 `useP2PMessages.test.ts` (call-ring routes to manager, skips chat/file). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5879/5879 (342 files).
- **Secrets in docs rotated to placeholders (P1)** (2026-09-15): `docs/DEPLOY.md` admin credentials (`ADMIN_USER=admin`, real admin password) → `<admin-user>` / `<strong-random-password>` + rotate-before-use note. Other matches = placeholders/example values, no real credentials.

- **Full lifecycle security integration test (S6)** (2026-09-14): new `src/lib/p2p/securityLifecycle.test.ts` (6 tests) exercises the whole security chain end-to-end against real crypto primitives (isolated in-memory `idb-keyval` mock so `masterKey`/`deviceSecurity`/`identityPin` run real flows): **register** — `getMasterKeySet()` deterministic 32B Ed25519 public + 64B secret pair; **pair** — DH public key signed by the identity verifies via `verifyDhSignature`, forged signature rejected; **verify/pin** — TOFU `verifyOrPinPeer` pins first-seen identity, rejects an attacker presenting a valid self-signed identity under the same peer id (pin unchanged), re-verify of the original identity still accepted; **encrypt** — `deriveSharedSessionKeys` yields identical HMAC + AES keys on both X25519 sides (64-hex, never transmitted), AES-GCM round-trip, tampered ciphertext rejected, decryption with a third-party session key rejected; **safety number** — deterministic, order-independent (sorted concatenation), differs between identities; **reconnect** — `handleWsClose` backoff sequence `[1000,2000,4000,8000,16000,30000,30000,30000,30000,30000]`, stops after `maxReconnectAttempts=10` (no further timer), counter resets to 0 on re-register. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5878/5878 (342 files).
- **Identity verification UI wired to real keys (S5)** (2026-09-14): Verify Security button in `ContactProfileModal` was dead — it opened `SafetyNumberModal` without a local identity key (`myPeerId` never passed from `ContactsView`), so the safety number never computed (0 groups, blank screen). Now the profile resolves both real identity keys itself: local Ed25519 public key via `getMasterKeySet()` and the peer's TOFU-pinned identity via `getPinnedIdentity(contact.id)`; `SafetyNumberModal` computes the safety number only when BOTH identity keys exist, shows an honest unverified state (no number, gray level 0) until the peer identity is pinned, and `theirId` falls back to the contact id when unpinned. Regression: new `SafetyNumberModal.test.tsx` (5: groups render with both keys, unverified without pinned key, theirId fallback/pinned, close). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5872/5872 (341 files).
- **Signaling reconnect backoff + peer directory (S4)** (2026-09-14): `P2PTransport.handleWsClose` reconnect switched from fixed 1s×attempt linear (5 tries, 5s cap) to the house exponential pattern — `min(1000 × 2^(attempts−1), 30000)`, 10 attempts (identical to `signaling/manager.ts`) — so a signaling outage no longer causes reconnect thundering herds. New REST `GET /api/peers/<key>` on the signaling relay returns `{key, online}` from the live `clients` map — single-key lookup only (no batch/list, no enumeration oracle for presence tracking); key validated with the register charset (≤128, `[A-Za-z0-9+/=_:.-]`); lets a client probe a peer before burning a WebSocket offer. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5867/5867 (340 files).
- **Master seed fail-closed storage (S3)** (2026-09-14): `storeMasterSeed` no longer degrades to plaintext IndexedDB when the device-bound key is unavailable — it now throws (`refusing to persist plaintext seed`). Root key material at rest is always `enc:v1:`-wrapped; browsers always derive a fingerprint key so identity boot is unaffected, and legacy-plaintext migration (already try/catch'd) keeps the old value readable until re-wrapped. Regression: `masterKey.test.ts` — per-test stable mock key + idb-store reset (encrypt/decrypt same key), +1 test (fail-closed, no plaintext write); gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5867/5867 (340 files).
- **Signaling broadcast size caps (S2)** (2026-09-14): `publish`/`presence`/`notify` now require a non-null `data` and reject serialized payloads >64 KB (`MAX_BROADCAST_DATA_SIZE`) with `Data too large` — broadcast fan-out (1 sender → N subscribers) previously amplified any payload up to the WS `maxPayload` (1 MB) per recipient; roster/presence/notify/embed-channel payloads are all ≪64 KB today. Gates: tsc clean, vitest 5866/5866 (340 files).
- **Signaling anti-replay + strict frame validation (S1)** (2026-09-14): `offer`/`answer`/`ice-candidate` now carry a monotonic `seq` (client `sendSignaling` auto-increments; server relays `seq`, rejects `seq <= lastSeen` per sender — `senderSeqs` map — and clears on disconnect; receiver drops stale `answer`/`ice-candidate` frames, a fresh `offer` resets the per-sender tracker for legitimate renegotiation). Server strict-frame checks added: SDP must be an object with `sdp` string ≤256 KB and `type` ∈ {`offer`,`answer`}; ICE candidate must be an object with `candidate` string ≤4 KB (both bounded server-side, defense-in-depth on top of the WS `maxPayload`). Regression: +3 `P2PTransport.test.ts` (stale-drop, monotonic-increase + fresh-offer reset, seq attachment); gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5866/5866 (340 files).
- **P2P frame identity and chat-context binding** (2026-09-14): authenticated data frames now carry the sender public-key context and are rejected when it does not match the authenticated remote peer. Incoming chat messages route by exact `chatId` before legacy display-name fallback, preventing same-name chats from receiving each other's messages. Regression: full suite 5863/5863, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **Monotonic sequence counters for P2P data frames** (2026-09-14): new outbound frames carry a session-scoped sequence number inside the authenticated envelope; receivers reject stale or repeated sequence values before decrypt/dispatch. Counters reset on session setup/disconnect, and legacy frames without a sequence remain readable during migration. Regression: P2P focused tests 56/56, full suite 5862/5862, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **Ordered P2P receive processing** (2026-09-14): authenticated data-channel frames now pass through a per-transport promise chain, preserving wire order while HMAC verification and AES-GCM decryption are asynchronous. Receive errors are isolated so one malformed frame cannot break later delivery. Regression: P2P focused tests 56/56, full suite 5862/5862, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **P2P anti-replay cache for encrypted frames** (2026-09-14): `P2PTransport` now drops an already accepted AES-GCM payload during the same session, keeps the replay cache bounded at 2048 entries, and clears it on disconnect. Regression covers duplicate authenticated frame delivery; full suite 5862/5862, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **Peer-confirmed read receipts for open chats** (2026-09-14): when a direct chat is open and scrolled to the bottom, the client sends a `chat-read` frame for the latest incoming message; the sender changes only the matching outgoing message to `read`. Read receipts are deduplicated per message and disabled for channels. Regression: full suite 5861/5861, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **Peer-confirmed delivery ACKs for chat text** (2026-09-14): `msg1:` frames now carry a stable `messageId`; the receiving peer sends a `chat-ack`, and the sender changes only the matching outgoing message to `delivered`. Legacy frames without `messageId` remain readable via timestamp fallback. ACKs use the existing encrypted P2P data path rather than plaintext signaling metadata. Regression: full suite 5860/5860, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **P2P file delivery status made truthful** (2026-09-14): file transfers now start as `queued`, skip transport while offline, propagate broadcast failures instead of swallowing them, and become `sent` only after meta/chunks/end are accepted by the P2P broadcast path. Regression: `useFileSend.test.ts` 5/5; lint and TypeScript clean.
- **P2P text delivery now uses the live transport** (2026-09-14): text messages are encoded as `msg1:` frames and sent through `p2pNetwork.broadcast()`; `sent` is assigned only after a connected data channel accepts the payload, while no-peer/offline messages remain `queued`. `P2PTransport.send()` now rejects when its channel is unavailable, and `P2PNetwork.broadcast()` rejects when no peer is connected instead of silently dropping data. Durable queue flush retries text only after a real broadcast succeeds. Regression: focused P2P/message tests 58/58, full suite 5859/5859, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **P2P delivery status no longer lies on reconnect** (2026-09-14): removed both online-event flush paths that marked every IndexedDB-queued message as `sent` without calling a transport or receiving an acknowledgement. Messages remain `queued` until the transport-owned send/ack pipeline is wired; queue tests now enforce this contract. Focused regression: 38/38; TypeScript clean.
- **Chat/call UX audit: unread state, jump counter, preview controls, voice hold** (2026-09-14): opening a chat now clears its unread count before navigation; the jump-to-bottom action clears the "new messages while scrolled up" counter; preview calls no longer render mute/video/screen-share/record controls without a live media stream; voice-note start no longer runs twice on pointer-down plus click and keeps keyboard Enter/Space access. Regression: focused chat/call tests 42/42, full unit suite 5859/5859, lint and TypeScript clean.
- **`navigateTo`/`pushView` duplicate drill-down prop merged (§1.3)** (2026-09-14): `App.tsx` passed the same `pushView` function under two names — `AppShell` used `navigateTo` (bot open, premium CTA) and forwarded `pushView` to `AppMainContent` → `FeatureViews`. `navigateTo` removed: interface prop, destructure, both call sites now use `pushView`. Zero behavior change (identical function, fallbacks preserved). Regression: `AppShell.test.tsx` + `FeatureViews.test.tsx` 26/26, full suite 5858/5858 (340 files).

### Added
- **Mobile chat: swipe right to close (Telegram back-gesture)** (2026-09-14): full-screen mobile chat (`ChatPreviewLayer.tsx` root, `md:z-40` → filled pane) now closes by swiping right — drag on the header/composer/empty areas (message bubbles keep their own swipe-to-reply drag, innermost drag wins; vertical pan untouched). `drag={isMobile && !selectionMode && !showStickerPicker && !showMediaPanel ? "x" : false}`, constraints `{left:0,right:0}`, elastic `{left:0,right:0.35}`, close on `offset.x > 90 || velocity.x > 600` via existing `onClose`; disabled during multi-select, sticker picker and media panel. `useIsMobile` imported direct from `../hooks/useMediaQuery` (not in hooks barrel). Exit animation already slides right — direction matches. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5858/5858 (340 files).
- **UX: chat bubble keeps its width, actions wrap instead of squeezing text** (2026-09-14): `daa1592` moved `BubbleActions`/`MessageReactions` out of the bubble into the same flex row (`ChatMessage.tsx`); with default `flex-shrink:1` the `w-fit` bubble shrank next to actions (2×44px) + reaction chips, so mid-length messages wrapped earlier than Telegram. Fix: row `flex-wrap` + bubble `shrink-0` — message text keeps its natural wrap point, actions/reactions wrap to the next line only when the row is truly full (Telegram pattern). `.tsx`-only, handlers untouched. Regression: `ChatMessage.test.tsx` green (44 tests incl. CrmView). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5855/5855 (338 files).

- **P2P file transfer: chunked send / receive / IndexedDB persistence / sha256 integrity** (2026-09-14): chat file attachments now travel peer-to-peer, no server relay. (1) New `src/lib/fileTransfer/`: `chunker.ts` (size-based chunking: <1MB→64KB, <100MB→256KB, <1GB→1MB, else→4MB; a 3-byte file → 1 chunk), `frames.ts` (wire: `FTR_MAGIC='ftr1:'` frames `meta`/`chunk`/`end` + `MSG_MAGIC='msg1:'` chat-text frames, all wrapped in `BroadcastMessage { senderId, data, timestamp, messageId }`), `fileStore.ts` (IndexedDB: in-flight `meta` + reassembled chunks + final blob, resume-safe), `integrity.ts` (sha256Hex). (2) New hooks: `useFileSend.ts` (limit check — free 50MB / premium 500MB via `config/premium.ts` — → type check via `allowedFileTypes.ts` → sha256 → chunk → frame → send, toast on failure), `useP2PMessages.ts` (frame demux: ftr `meta`/`chunk`/`end` → reassemble + persist + progress, `msg1` → chat text; messageId dedupe), `useP2PBoot.ts` (session bootstrap); wired at App mount. (3) `AttachmentMedia.tsx` receive path (blob → object URL). Regression: new `useFileSend.test.ts` (5), `useP2PMessages.test.ts` (6), `AttachmentMedia.test.tsx` receive cases (8 total). i18n ×8: `fileTransfer.*` block + `allTests.test.ts` required-keys. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5855/5855 (338 files).
- **Free second signalling host: Cloudflare Workers reverse proxy** (2026-09-12): single-host app (`wss://mess.cvr.name/ws`) has no fallback when the one domain is DNS-hijacked / geo-poisoned / unroutable under a VPN — and no second host is rented. New `server/signalling-proxy-worker.mjs` re-exposes `mess.cvr.name` on Cloudflare's free tier (100k req/day): proxies both the WebSocket upgrade (`/ws`) and the REST relay-token endpoint (`/api/auth/*`, `Host` header remapped for origin vhost routing). Client already rotates to it: `SignallingPool.getNextAvailable` picks the next seed once the primary fails, and `relayToken.ts` derives the REST base from `SIGNALING_SEED_URLS[0]`, so the seed list is swapped at build time — no app-code change. Deploy: `wrangler deploy server/signalling-proxy-worker.mjs`, then build with `VITE_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS="wss://mess.cvr.name/ws,wss://<worker>.workers.dev/ws"`. `signalling.ts` config doc extended. D1–D5: 0 findings (no UI changes).
- **Cloudflare relay proxy wired into `deploy-all.ps1`** (2026-09-12): one-command deploys now opt into the second seed. `-RelayProxyHost <host>` (or `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID` → `$RelayProxyName.$env:CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID.workers.dev`) injects `VITE_SIGNALING_SEED_URLS="wss://mess.cvr.name/ws,wss://<host>/ws"` into the SPA/native build (Vite bakes VITE_* at build time — step runs before `npm run build`). With `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` set, the worker itself is deployed first via `npx wrangler@latest deploy server/signalling-proxy-worker.mjs --name $RelayProxyName` (skip when token absent — warn, build still references the host). New `-RelayProxyName` (default `signalling-relay-fallback`) + `-SkipRelayProxy` switches; Help text updated. Regression: `deploy-all.ps1` PowerShell parse OK.
- **Serverless LAN pairing proof-of-concept (settings → LAN Mesh)** (2026-09-12): zero-server WebRTC for two app instances on the same network — invite QR (offer) + answer QR, no signaling server involved. `P2PTransport` gained a pairing mode: `enablePairingMode()` + `createPairingOffer()` / `acceptPairingOffer()` / `acceptPairingAnswer()` carry the SDP + ephemeral ECDH (X25519) in a `mess-lan/1:` payload outside the WS channel — ICE candidates are gathered locally (extended `onicecandidate` pairing branch, `localCandidates` + gather-complete resolve, 5s cap) instead of being relayed; session keys derive identically to the relay path (`deriveSessionFromPeerDh`, AES-GCM + HMAC preserved, identity pinning when `identityPub`/`dhSig` present, fail-closed otherwise). `P2PTransport.test.ts` extended with a two-transport loopback harness (MockRTCPeerConnection offer/answer/candidate bridging) covering both roles, fail-closed, key derivation, and `hasSessionKeys()`. New `settings/MeshSection.tsx`: three-step UI (create invite QR → paste invite → generate answer QR → paste answer → connect) + live send-test + received view + reset; strings use `t(key, enFallback)` so no x8 locale files touched (gate stays green). Wired as lazy `mesh` section in `SettingsSectionContent.tsx` + menu row in `SettingsMainMenu.tsx` (Radar icon). Regression: new `MeshSection.test.tsx` (6u: steps render, create-invite live-starts transport + payload, answer flow paste→enabled→accepts, connect flow, invalid invite → error + cleared answer, transports torn down on unmount). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (every control live: invite→`createPairingOffer`, answer→`acceptPairingOffer`, connect→`acceptPairingAnswer`, send→`send`, reset→`disconnect`; no synthetic ids, no empty handlers). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5784/5784 (334 files).

- **Back button returns to origin across views (CRM → Premium, list → bot, bot → miniApp)** (2026-09-14): in-app Back from Premium opened via CRM/company reset to the settings main menu (`setSubView(null)`), dropping the caller — same hard-coded jump for bot Back (`→ bots`) and miniApp Close (`→ bot`). New `navOrigin` state in `App.tsx`: cross-view drill-downs record `{view, subView}` via new `pushView()` (wired: AppShell side-list Premium CTA, company `CrmView` CTA, chat-list `onOpenBot`, bot `onOpenMiniApp`); `goBack()` restores the recorded origin and clears it, falling back to `chats` when empty; top-level navigation (`handleNavigate`) clears the origin. `FeatureViews` premium/Bot/miniApp Back + Close now use `goBack()` (fallbacks preserve old behavior when the prop is absent); settings-internal subviews (recordings/callLog/radar) untouched — they already return to settings main, which IS their origin. Regression: `FeatureViews.test.tsx` +4 (premium back → `goBack`, company CTA → `pushView('settings','premium')`, bot back → `goBack`, bot → miniApp `pushView('miniApp')`).
