- **Landing: raw HTML tags shown instead of bold/italic markup** (2026-09-14): security fix `d1537ab` routed every i18n value through `escapeHtml()` before `innerHTML`, but the landing translations intentionally contain `<b>/<em>/<br>` markup — so `hero.title` etc. rendered the literal tags (`<b>Mess&Anger</b>`) on the page. Fix in `public/landing/app.js`: new allowlist-sanitizer `i18nHtml()` — keeps `b/em/strong/br` (rejecting any tag with `on*=`/`javascript:`/`style=` attributes), escapes everything else, normalizes `\n` escapes (double-escaped in the lang JSONs) + real newlines → `<br>`. Untrusted `ticker.json`/`releases.json` sinks keep `escapeHtml()` (never allowlisted). `node --check` clean.
- **Tauri MSI `&` in product name: `msi` target removed from default build** (2026-09-14): `productName: "Mess&Anger"` contains a raw `&`; the Tauri 2.x bundler renders `main.wxs` with handlebars `no_escape`, so the ampersand lands as a raw XML byte and candle.exe fails to parse — every Windows `tauri build` died non-zero at the MSI step. Fix in `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`: `msi` dropped from `bundle.targets` — MSI is now produced solely by the existing rescue builder `scripts/build-windows-msi.mjs` (escapes the bare ampersands in the generated `main.wxs`, rewrites `locale.wxl` with the escaped product name, runs the exact candle/light commands, writes the MSI to `src-tauri/target/<triple>/release/bundle/msi/` where `scripts/release.mjs` picks it up). Workflow: `tauri build` (exit 0: nsis + rest) → `tauri build --bundles msi` (renders the wix dir; candle failure expected) → `node scripts/build-windows-msi.mjs`. Config-only, no app code touched.
- **Profile/modal windows escaped the chat-pane stacking context (z-index overlap bug)** (2026-09-13): request with screenshot — «профиль (окно) перекрывается интерфейсом». Root cause: `ChatPreviewLayer.tsx` opens the chat as a `motion.div` spring slide (`initial={{ x: 40, scale: 0.95 }}`), and a running CSS `transform` makes that div a **stacking context**. Every fullscreen `position:fixed` overlay rendered *inside* it (`ContactProfileModal`, `ChatProfileView`, `MediaViewer`, all `fixed inset-0 z-[var(--z-modal)]`, wired via `ChatPreviewOverlays`) resolved `fixed` like `absolute` against the chat-pane box and trapped at `z-50` — the same z as the mobile `BottomNav`, which renders later in the DOM and painted *above* the modal. Fix (house portal pattern, cf. `ui/Modal.tsx` / `MessageContextMenu.tsx`): all three modals now `createPortal(…, document.body)` — they mount at body level, outside any transform, so `fixed inset-0` is viewport-relative again and `--z-modal` (500) beats `z-50` nav. `SavedMessagesPanel`/`ChannelCommentsView` intentionally left `absolute` (they fill the chat pane, not the screen). `ConfirmDialog`/`InviteQRModal` nested inside already self-portal. The `beforeinstallprompt` console line the user saw (`Banner not shown: …preventDefault() called`) is the standard Chrome informational message for the deferred-install pattern (`useInstallPrompt.ts` calls `prompt()` on demand) — not a bug, no change. Regression: `ChatProfileView.test.tsx` +1 (portal test: overlay renders as a `document.body` child carrying `fixed inset-0 z-[var(--z-modal)]`, i.e. cannot be an ancestor-transformed trap). D1–D5: 0 findings (control handlers untouched). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5805/5805 (332 files).
- **Systemic: 10 more fixed-fullscreen overlays portaled to body — same stacking-context bug family as chat-pane modals** (2026-09-13): follow-up scan found a *second* ancestor trap: `app/ContentView.tsx` wraps all main content in a `motion.div` whose y-variants (`enter/exit {opacity:0, y:40}`) run a transform AND `className="relative z-20"` — a positioned element with z-index creates a stacking context, so every `position:fixed` overlay mounted inside main content (via `AppMainContent`) was bounded at z-20, below app chrome (`AppSideList z-30`, `BottomNav z-50`, mobile `ChatPreviewLayer z-50`). Now portaled via `createPortal(…, document.body)` (house pattern): `stories/StoryViewer`, `stories/StoryComposer`, `GlobalSearch`, `chat-preview/ChatContextMenu` (bottom sheet), `recordings/RecordingPlayer`, `crm/CrmImportWizard`, `payments/ChatPickerModal`, `settings/ShareIdentityModal`, `crm/CrmModal` (base for CrmTasks/CrmInviteModal/CrmDealModal/ContactCard/AcceptInviteModal — one portal fixes all five), and `chat-preview/NotificationCenter` (only the popover+backdrop fragment portals; the bell button stays in the list header, and its `getBoundingClientRect`-derived `style{{top,right}}` is viewport-relative so portal-safe). Root-level trees already safe (no change): `CallOverlay`, `AppOverlays`, plus `Modal`/`FormModal`/`InviteQRModal`/`ConfirmModal` self-portals. Regression: +2 portal tests (StoryViewer dialog + NotificationCenter popover live in `document.body`, bell stays put); re-targeted 13 container-query tests (StoryViewer ×10 helper → `document.body`, ChatContextMenu backdrop, ChatPickerModal overlay+panel). D1–D5: 0 findings (CSS-only moves + fragment split, handlers untouched). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest **5807/5807 (332 files)**.

- **Telegram-style gestures app-wide: MediaViewer pinch/pan/double-tap/dismiss, mirrored chat swipe-reply, stories swipe nav + dismiss** (2026-09-13): request «продумать жесты для кнопок и всего остального (как это сделано в Телеграме)». (1) **MediaViewer.tsx** — gesture layer rebuilt: unified `gestureRef { mode: idle|nav|pan|pinch|dismiss, startX/Y, startDist, startScale, startOffset, moved }` + `scaleRef` + `lastTapRef`; pinch (2-touch dist-ratio, scale clamp 1..5, offset cleared at 1), pan (clamped bounds `(viewport·scale−viewport)/2`), horizontal swipe nav (`|dx|≥60 ∧ |dx|>|dy|·1.5`), swipe-down dismiss (`|dy|>110 ∧ |dy|>|dx|·1.5`, live follow during move, promote from nav at `|dy|>90`), double-tap zoom toggle (1↔2.5 within 300ms) + `onDoubleClick` on photo, keyboard −/+ zoom (0.5..4), **zoom resets on media switch** (was leaking scale across photos), `data-zoom` test hook. (2) **ChatMessage.tsx** — outgoing (`isMe`) messages now swipe **left** to reply (was impossible): `dragConstraints` mirrored `{left:-80,right:0}`, `onDrag` → `offset.x < −10 ? msg.id : null`, `onDragEnd` → `offset.x < −60 ? onReply(msg)`, right-edge accent indicator (incoming keeps left-edge); `drag={selectionMode ? false : "x"}` (drag disabled while bulk-selecting). (3) **StoryContent.tsx/StoryViewer.tsx** — horizontal swipe nav (left→next via `dx < −80`, right→prev via `dx > 80`) + swipe-down close (`|dy|>120 ∧ |dy|>|dx|·1.5`) with live `moved` tracking (`hypot>8`), `touch-pan-x` to preserve native horizontal scroll feel, and a `swipeConsumedRef` that suppresses the browser-synthesized click after a swipe so a swipe can't also trigger tap-zone nav; touch-hold pause wiring (`onPauseStart/End`) preserved through the new touch handlers. Gesture map (Telegram parity): chat list swipe-actions (existing), message swipe-reply both sides + indicator (new), media full-screen pinch/pan/double-tap/dismiss (new), stories swipe nav + dismiss (new). Regression: `MediaViewer.test.tsx` 7→10 (dismiss via vertical swipe, double-tap zoom in/out, pinch zoom, zoom reset on media switch), `ChatMessage.test.tsx` 21→27 (motion mock now captures `h.motionProps`; mirrored constraints, drag-sign, short-swipe no-reply, selectionMode drag `false`, indicator edge placement both sides + hide on non-match), `StoryViewer.test.tsx` 22→26 (swipe-left→next story, swipe-right→prev user, swipe-down→close, swipe click-suppress). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (all handlers live — swipe targets are the same reply/nav/close actions already wired; no synthetic ids, no echo writes, no empty handlers). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5803/5803 (334 files; 1 transient `RecoveryManager` `vi.waitFor` timeout under full-suite load — green 5803/5803 on rerun and 6/6 in isolation).

- **Deploy-gate flake fixed: `RecoveryManager.test.ts` concurrency waits outlived by real PBKDF2-600k under parallel suite load** (2026-09-13): `deploy-all.ps1` vitest phase failed twice (10:05 this session, earlier) at `vi.waitFor(() => expect(storeMasterSeed).toHaveBeenCalled())` — default `waitFor` timeout is 1000ms, and `restoreFromPhrase`/`generateRecoveryPhrase` run a real 600k-iteration PBKDF2 (RecoveryManager.ts:46-51) *before* the persisted-seed assertion is reachable; with 334 files loading in parallel, WebCrypto derivation occasionally exceeds 1s. Both concurrency tests now use `it(name, { timeout: 30000 }, …)` + `vi.waitFor(…, { timeout: 20000 })` — assertion intent (seed + device-key persist together, restore resolves only after seed write) is unchanged, real crypto kept, only the wait budget is realistic for CI. Regression: file 4/4 isolated runs green + full suite **5803/5803 (334 files)**. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean.
- **Account restore UX: backup import at login + honest Cloud Sync relabel + phrase-only warning** (2026-09-13): request «почему вначале восстановление происходит только с помощью секретной фразы (на другом устройстве?) — может сделать загрузку из резервной копии… продумай хорошо логику восстановления аккаунта… посмотри как работает облачное сохранение — непонятно куда сохраняется и сохраняется ли вообще». Root causes found: the recovery phrase restores only identity keys (deterministic from mnemonic entropy) — chats/contacts never came back on a new device and nothing said so; a real cross-device backup file already existed (`Settings → Backup & Export`) but was never offered at onboarding; Cloud Sync was a **local-only IndexedDB snapshot** (`app_cloud_sync_snapshot`, written by `saveCloudSyncSnapshot` but read by **zero** callers) whose subtitle falsely claimed "Sync chats and settings between devices". (1) **`LoginScreen.tsx`** — new `import-data` step between phrase restore and PIN setup: amber warning box on the phrase step (`auth.login.phraseOnlyNote`: «Секретная фраза восстанавливает только личность и ключи. Чаты и контакты восстанавливаются из файла резервной копии»); after successful `restoreFromPhrase` → «Restore your data» step with Import button (hidden `input[type=file] accept=".enc,.json"`, `isEncryptedBackup` magic-byte sniff → encrypted = `TextInputModal` password prompt → `decryptBackupFile`, plain JSON = `parseBackupFile` → both route through the existing `applyBackup(data)` (store setters + all IDB writes + `markDataHydrated`), busy spinner, `role="alert"` inline error on import/password failure, `Import failed. Check the password or file and try again.`) + «Start fresh» secondary → proceeds to PIN setup. Reuses `lib/backup.ts` — no crypto duplicated. (2) **Cloud Sync honesty** — `cloudSyncOption` → 'Local data snapshot', `cloudSyncSubtitle` → 'Stored on this device only. Not synced to the cloud.' in all 8 locales (en/ru/de/es/fr/ja/ko/zh); toggle now honestly labels the local snapshot. (3) i18n ×8: 9 new `auth.login.*` keys (phraseOnlyNote, restoreDataTitle/Subtitle, importBackup, importing, startFresh, importFailed, importPasswordTitle/Placeholder). - **Load speed: nginx gzip_static (build-time .gz served verbatim) + idle post-boot prefetch lane** (2026-09-13): §4.4/LCP. (1) **The web was served uncompressed**: nginx on the VPS had no gzip directives, while `vite-plugin-compression` already shipped `.gz`+`.br` beside every hashed asset — the main bundle (316.7KB raw) went over the wire fully raw. `server/mess.cvr.name.conf` now turns on `gzip_static on` (serves the pre-built `.gz` verbatim, 0 CPU) + `gzip on` (dynamic fallback, level 5, min 256, `gzip_proxied any`) for JS/CSS/JSON/SVG/manifest/plain and an explicit `add_header Vary "Accept-Encoding" always` (the `.br` files are ignored — `ngx_http_brotli_static` is not installed; node `/admin` already prefers `.br`). Live-verified: `index-*.js` now serves 100,351 bytes `content-encoding: gzip` (was 316.69KB raw ≈ −68%). Duplicate-MIME warning removed (`text/html` dropped from `gzip_types` — compiled-in), `gzip_vary` dropped to avoid double `Vary`. Deployed via scp → `sudo cp` → `nginx -t` → `systemctl reload`; only pre-existing warning = `http2` deprecation in the *other* `cvr.name.conf`. (2) **Post-boot prefetch** (`src/main.tsx`): after existing idle preload (theme+sounds) a `warmPostBootLane` runs in `requestIdleCallback` (timeout 6000, `setTimeout` 500 fallback) warming `RegistrationScreen`, `LoginScreen` (lazy in `AppAuthGate`) and `ChatPreviewLayer` (108.9KB r / 25.6KB br chunk, lazy in `ActiveChatWorkspace`) — dynamic `import()` specifiers resolve to the existing lazy chunks (dedupe by module id), so first interaction on auth + chat opens near-instantly after idle. Verified: dist/index.html still exactly 6 `modulepreload` links, entry still 316.69KB / 81.57KB br — **critical path unchanged**. `otpauth` NOT removed (false positive — live in `server/auth.ts` admin 2FA). Source already fixed in git (commit `6624f4c`); rebuilt `admin/` from restored source (`admin/src/lib/i18n.tsx` has preload + ready guard), deployed to `/home/user0/messanger/dist/admin/` (stale nested `dist/admin/admin/` + old chunk assets removed; old `index-D_5tyr_m.js` → 404). Verified: `index-CZ7xuIS_.js` + `en-TMRgQHr1.js` + `ru-oB8WhqNA.js` + CSS all 200; old bundle/nested dir 404. tsc + vite build clean.
- **`/admin` 502 Bad Gateway (mess.cvr.name) — dead PHP-FPM socket + unwired admin routes** (2026-09-13): user reported `https://mess.cvr.name/admin` → nginx 502. Root cause: (1) the site nginx config pointed `fastcgi_pass unix:/var/run/php/php8.2-fpm.sock`, but PHP 8.2 was removed from the VPS during system upgrade (only 8.3/8.4 remain; live pool socket = `php8.3-fpm-mess.cvr.name.sock`). The webroot is a pure static SPA with no `index.php`, so every non-static path fell to `try_files ... /index.php` → fastcgi connect to missing socket → 502; `/` still 200 via static `index.html`. (2) The admin SPA (`$AppRoot/dist/admin`, served by `serveAdminFile` on REST 3003) had no nginx `location`, so `/admin` always cascaded into the PHP trap. (3) `handleAdminRoute` (server/routes/admin.ts) was exported but never imported into `server/signaling-server.ts` → even with a live SPA, `/api/admin/*` returned 404. Fixes: `server/mess.cvr.name.conf` — fastcgi socket re-pointed to php8.3 mess pool + `location = /admin` and `location ^~ /admin/` → `proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3003`; `server/signaling-server.ts` — imported `handleAdminRoute` and added it to the REST handler chain (after `handleIntegrationsRoute`, before fallback 404). Deployed: scp server files → `pm2 restart mess-signaling --update-env` (health 200), `sudo cp` nginx conf → `nginx -t` OK → reload. Verified via public https: `/admin` 200, `/admin/` 200, `/api/admin/users` 401 (auth gate), `/health` 200, `/` 200. tsc clean (no server tsconfig; root `tsc --noEmit` clean). D1–D5: no UI controls touched.
- **Google Play pre-launch compliance: edge-to-edge, PiP, orientation lock, R8** (2026-09-13): 5 pre-launch warnings closed. (1) **Edge-to-edge**: `displayOverride: ["edge-to-edge"]` added to `TwaManifest` (build never passes it — `scripts/build-android.mjs` now sets it; PWA already ships `viewport-fit=cover` + `env(safe-area-inset-*)` insets). (2) **Picture-in-Picture**: new `patchManifest()` post-generation step in `build-android.mjs` — bubblewrap doesn't emit PiP attributes; adds `android:supportsPictureInPicture="true"` + `android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|smallestScreenSize|keyboardHidden"` to `LauncherActivity`. (3) **Size/orientation restriction for large screens**: `"orientation": "any"` in `config/android-publish.json` (was `portrait`; source of truth feeds `scripts/build-android.mjs`) + `config/android-publish.json` sync; `public/manifest.json` `orientation` `portrait-primary` → `any`. (4) **R8**: `android/app/build.gradle` release buildType now `shrinkResources true` + `proguardFiles getDefaultProguardFile('proguard-android-optimize.txt'), 'proguard-rules.pro'` (new file keeps `com.messanger.e2e.**` + `com.google.androidbrowserhelper.**` — manifest-referenced classes). (5) **Memory/perf**: covered by R8 resource+code shrinking. `patchManifest()` also adds `android:resizeableActivity="true"` to `<application>` (large-screen windowed/resize support). Run order in `build-android.mjs`: `createProject()` (regenerates manifest) → `patchManifest()` (idempotent single-pass on fresh output) → `generate-assetlinks` → build; `node --check` + `@bubblewrap/core` `TwaManifest` accept `displayOverride:["edge-to-edge"]` + `orientation:"any"` verified. D1–D5: no UI controls touched. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5767/5767 (332 files).

The `hooks/index.ts` barrel itself has exactly one live consumer (`AppMainContent.tsx` imports only `useKeyboardScroll`). `getErrorStats` in `src/lib/errorHandling.ts` was consumed only by the deleted `useErrorStats`; removed it + barrel entry + its test + the dead `getErrorStats` mock in `useGlobalErrorHandler.test.ts`. "X" : "X"` ternary branches semantically distinct — 0 byte-identical). - **`deploy-all.ps1` web upload: silent data loss from Windows bsdtar "Couldn't visit directory"** (2026-09-12): on the prod deploy the archive dropped real files (`install-ios.html` was missing from the live webroot after a "✓ Web files uploaded" pass). Root cause: Windows bsdtar intermittently fails to "visit" a directory mid-read (av/lock/indexer churn) and can silently skip those entries — including at rc=0 — and the old `tar cf - . | ssh "tar xf -"` pipe drowned the failure in ssh's exit code. Fix in `scripts/deploy-all.ps1` phase [4/5]: (1) archive to a local file with an **explicit per-entry file list** (`tar -cf -T list`, no directory recursion → no visit-failure mode); (2) authoritatively diff `tar tf <file>` against the local file set (regexp-free `./` prefix strip); retry ×4 only when the diff is non-empty; (3) ship via `ssh "cat > _incoming.tar && tar xf <name> -C $WebRoot --overwrite && rm && echo EXTRACT_OK"` so ssh's exit code reflects the real extraction, then verify `index.html/install-ios.html/offline.html/sw.js/manifest.json` exist remotely (throw otherwise). Deploy-verified: archive = 576/576 files, remote `install-ios.html` present, HTTPS 200. CAUTION: a naive `--exclude=./server` + `.TrimStart('./')` comparison silently mismatches dot-entries (`.well-known/*` → `well-known/*`); the fix uses substring strip instead.

- **`install-ios.html` missing from fresh builds: generated after web upload** (2026-09-12): a fresh `npm run build` wiped the stale `dist/install-ios.html` left by a previous iOS run, and `build-ios.mjs` runs in the *last* deploy phase (after web upload) — so the post-upload verification (`install-ios.html missing from /var/www/mess.cvr.name`) correctly caught a file that could never be uploaded again. Fix: static `public/install-ios.html` added (Vite copies `public/` into `dist/` on every build, so web deploys always ship it); `scripts/build-ios.mjs` now copies it from `public/` when present (else falls back to the inline template), and the template + generated file stay byte-identical. Regression = the post-upload `must` list check in `deploy-all.ps1` + `node --check` on the edited script; deploy-verified file present with rebuilt webroot.

- **Messenger connectivity under VPN: reconnect storm + blocked detection + visible error** (2026-09-12): (1) `SignallingManager` misread server rejections as transient blips — on `1008 "Too many connections"` / `"Origin not allowed"` / `"Authentication required"` it reconnected with exp backoff, and each attempt counted against the server's per-IP connection budget. Shared VPN egress IPs (many users behind one provider IP) exhausted it, producing a self-sustaining 1008 loop into `error`. Now `manager.ts` classifies close frames: {1008,1009,4401,4403} or reason-matches `/too many/i`,`/origin not allowed/i`,`/authentication required/i`,`/blocked/i`,`/rate.?limit/i` → `blocked` (+45s fixed slow retry, single client stays well under any per-minute cap and the UI shows Degraded instead of a reconnect firehose); anything else → transient (`disconnected`, exp backoff 1s..30s). (2) Dead-lock fix: a closed socket never left the manager at `connected`, but `connect()` early-returns on `connected`, so the reconnect timer ran a no-op and the link stayed dead until reload/online. `markTransient` now flips `connected|blocked → disconnected` before scheduling; error+close for the same socket dedupe into one timer (dual-timer storm fixed). (3) `WsTunnel` captures the server close frame (`getLastClose()`, `onClose` handler receives `{code, reason}`, no-frame closes normalised to 1006). (4) Visual: `useAppConnection` returns `connectionError` → `AppChrome` → `TransportIndicator` tooltip shows the raw server reason in `--danger` for `blocked`/`error` ("Closed by server … Too many connections" instead of a bare status dot). (5) Server: `MAX_CONNECTIONS_PER_MINUTE` 10 → 60 (10 too strict for shared-VPN egress IPs). Regression: `wsTunnel.test.ts` +3 (close info passed to handler, stored pre-handler, abrupt→1006), `manager.test.ts` +6 (1008→blocked + 45s slow retry, reason-based blocked, 1006→transient+backoff, dead-lock reconnect after close from `connected`, error+close dedupe → single socket, exponential backoff 1s→2s), `TransportIndicator.test.tsx` +4 (detail in tooltip for blocked/error, not shown when connected, absent when no detail). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (no new UI controls — tooltip detail only; empty handlers only in test mocks). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5771/5771 (332 files).
- **Appearance settings made live: font size, accents, chat background, density, message radius, animation intensity** (2026-09-12): (1) Font size was a no-op: `[data-font-size]` was set on the AppShell `<div>`, but the rule was pasted so the override applied to `--font-size-base` which is consumed by `html`/`body` — ancestors that resolve the `:root` default (16px) because custom properties inherit down. `useAppSettings` now mirrors `data-font-size` onto `documentElement` (alongside the existing theme mirror) so rem-based text/controls actually scale; `--control-font-size` pinned to an absolute `12px` so inputs don't drop below WCAG 12px when the base shrinks. (2) `Accent color` applied only after visiting the Appearance pane (mount effect): new `useAppearanceEffects` hook runs at app boot (`App.tsx`) syncing `--accent`, `--accent-bg`, `--accent-rgb` (gradient ambience + selection were stuck on the default) and `--accent-soft`; obsolete `AppearanceSettings` mount effect removed. (3) `Chat background`/`Message radius`/`Density`/`Animation intensity` had CSS writes with zero consumers: chat pane now reads `var(--chat-bg, var(--bg-secondary))` (new `[data-chat-bg=light/dark]` tokens + `[data-chat-bg=dots] .chat-surface` pattern, `ChatPreviewLayer`); bubbles carry a `msg-bubble` class overridden by `--message-radius`/`--message-radius-sm` (unlayered `.msg-bubble.rounded-*` rules preserve the grouped-message tail); `ChatListItem` padding/min-height + `ChatMessageList` padding read `--chat-item-pad-*`/`--message-list-pad-*` (`[data-density=compact]`); `AnimationContext` maps `low` → 0.15s, `high` → 0.3s duration, `off` → disabled. Regression: `useAppearanceEffects.test.tsx` new (3: defaults on html, re-apply on store change incl. radius+tail and invalid-hex guard), `useAppSettings.test.tsx` +1 (font size mirrored onto `documentElement`). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (no new interactive controls — CSS vars + hooks; animation buttons still fire `setAnimationIntensity`; `data-anim-intensity` now a real attr mirrored at boot). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5758/5758 (332 files).
- **Login/restore + PIN flows: same exposed hang pattern as registration** (2026-09-12): (1) `LoginScreen.tsx` `handleRestore` — `setStep("restoring")` was never called, so the "Restoring…" spinner block was unreachable dead UI (the identical dead-step bug fixed in `RegistrationScreen` on 09-12); heavy PBKDF2-600k phrase verification ran with a frozen screen. Now the restoring step shows immediately; on failure/throw the step reverts to `enter-phrase` so the error alert stays visible. (2) `RecoveryManager.restoreFromPhrase` — the persist finalize (`storeMasterSeed` → `deviceSecurity.storeMasterKeyHex`, itself a PBKDF2-600k fingerprint) now runs via `Promise.all` instead of serially, matching `generateRecoveryPhrase`. (3) `useAppLock.handleUnlock` — added an `unlockBusy` guard (PBKDF2-600k verify ≈255ms desktop) that blocks concurrent form submits and exposes a busy flag; `AppLockScreen` submit button now `disabled` with `…` while verifying; wired through `AppAuthGate`. (4) `SecuritySection` `handlePinSet`/`handlePinRemove` — added a `pinBusy` guard (a double-submit would run two concurrent 600k hashes); confirm button disabled + `…` while hashing. Regression: `LoginScreen.test.tsx` +2 (spinner shown while restoring, reverts to phrase entry on failure), `RecoveryManager.test.ts` +1 (restore persists seed + device key concurrently — gates the seed write, asserts fingerprint already started), `useAppLock.test.tsx` +1 (double `handleUnlock` → single verify), `AppLockScreen.test.tsx` +1 (busy → disabled + `…`), `SecuritySection.test.tsx` +1 (double submit while hashing → single hash, button disabled). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (new `disabled` states are busy guards, not dead controls; all set* inversions intact; empty handlers only in test mocks). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5754/5754 (331 files).
- **Registration "Create Identity" hang: spinner shown + parallel key derivation** (2026-09-12): (1) `RegistrationScreen.tsx` `handleGenerate` now calls `setStep("generating")` immediately — the "Generating secure keys…" spinner block existed but was unreachable dead UI, so the 2× PBKDF2-600k identity generation ran with a frozen screen; on error the step reverts to `welcome` so the error alert stays visible. (2) `RecoveryManager.generateRecoveryPhrase` runs the phrase-hash PBKDF2-600k concurrently with the persist chain (`storeMasterSeed(seed)` → `deviceSecurity.storeMasterKeyHex`, itself a PBKDF2-600k fingerprint derivation) instead of serially — roughly halves regeneration wall time (each PBKDF2 ≈255ms desktop, 1-3s mobile). Regression: `RegistrationScreen.test.tsx` +2 (spinner shown while generation pending, error reverts to welcome), `RecoveryManager.test.ts` gate test (does not resolve until seed + device key persisted — mocks updated to return Promises for the `.then` chain). D1–D5 re-scan: 0 findings (no new UI controls — generating step is a passive spinner; copy/skip/enter handlers all live; empty handlers only in test mocks). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5748/5748 (331 files).

### Fixed
- **Security audit fixes (S1–S12)** (2026-09-11): client-side security hardening from the settings/security audit. S1/S9 — `totpSecret`/`turnServerUrl`/`turnServerUser`/`turnServerPass` now persisted AES-256-GCM-encrypted in the privacy blob (`lib/securePersist.ts` value-level API, session key = device master key via `initAppStorage`; encrypted values lazily decrypted by `hydrateSecurePrivacyFields`). S2 — PIN hash iterations 100k → 600k (`hashAppLockPIN` default); new `cryptoCore.verifyAppLockPIN` keeps a legacy 100k fallback so existing hashes still unlock; `useAppLock` + `SecuritySection` PIN-remove switched to it. S3 — `updateSettings` mass-overwrite gated by `UPDATE_ALLOWLIST` (privacy toggles + TURN + currentLanguage/soundEnabled only; security-critical fields like `twoFactor`/`totpSecret`/`appLockHashedPIN` no longer writable this way). S5 — dead `components/lock/LockScreen.tsx` (duplicate unlock logic, no 2FA/biometric) removed + test + barrel + empty dir; live path = `AppLockScreen`. S8 — `twoFactor.generateSecret` Math.random fallback removed (throws if WebCrypto RNG unavailable). S10 — `secureWipe` now returns `{ok, warnings}` and `console.warn`s partial-wipe failures (was blanket noop). S11 — key-export passphrase reconfirm field (mismatch → blocked + `settings.keyRecovery.exportPassConfirm/exportPassMismatch` ×8 locales). Accepted unchanged (documented): S6 lock attempts stay in localStorage (clearing it also clears the PIN hash → lock off, no gain from IDB); S7 deviceSecurity `STATIC_SALT_HEX` stays (fingerprint salt, migration would orphan existing encrypted keys). Regression: `useAppLock.test.tsx` mock +`verifyAppLockPIN`; settings whitelist tests pass. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5707/5707 (332 files), audit prod 0 vulnerabilities.

### Fixed
- **Startup data-load speedups** (2026-09-10): `useDataHydration.ts` now runs all 7 IndexedDB reads in a single `Promise.all` (`chats_all`/`contacts_all`/`channels_all`/`bots_list`/`call_history_all` + `loadCompanyMessages()` + `loadCloudSyncMeta()` — was 5 parallel + 2 sequential awaits). `companySlice.loadCompanySettings()` parallelized from 8 sequential IDB reads to 2 batches (`Promise.all` on independent reads; group-key/channels/envelopes/channelKeys/siteChats stay gated on `companyId`, `joinCompanyChannel` fires right after `companyId` is set as before). `store/index.ts` data persistence switched from 6 synchronous IDB writes per state change to a coalescing write queue (write-in-flight tracker; burst changes collapse to the newest snapshot — `drainPersist` re-runs while a queued newer snapshot exists). Regression: new `index.test.ts` test proves a burst of 3 chat changes during a slow in-flight write yields exactly 2 `chats_all` writes (first + newest), intermediate state skipped. - **Settings dead rows removed, four dead settings wired** (2026-09-10): audit showed 16 settings that persisted with zero effect. HIDDEN (rows removed, slice fields + persist kept): proxy/torBridge/obfuscationMode/p2pMesh (`NetworkSection` → only obfuscationEnabled + relayBackend + autoReconnect + TURN input remain), visNumber/visActivity/profilePhotoVisibility/callsVisibility/messagesFrom (`PrivacySection`), deadMansSwitch (`SecuritySection` auto-wipe group), spamFilter (`SpamSection.tsx` deleted + barrel export), draftsEnabled/offlineMode/mediaAutoLoad (`StorageSection` → presentational, only encryption + clear-cache), pwaBanner + install-How-to (`AppearanceSettings`). - **Calls: video e2e coverage (Camera + Picture-in-picture)** (`e2e/calls.spec.ts` 11/11): "video call shows a live local camera preview and re-attaches after a toggle". Headless Chromium rejects all real `getUserMedia` (`NotSupportedError`), so the test stubs it via `page.addInitScript` with a synthetic `MediaStream` (animated canvas `captureStream(10)` video + silent `AudioContext` audio track). Covers the full video pipeline: accept-as-video → live PiP preview attached → control-bar auto-hide → stage re-show → video off (PiP removed) → video on (PiP re-attached) → end call.
- **Calls: network quality notification (Уведомление о качестве сети)**: while a call is `connected`, `CallManager` tracks the transport latency level (good <150ms / fair <400ms / poor) and emits `call:quality { level }` whenever it changes (first reading after (re)connect is the baseline, so the existing quality bar is not re-announced). `CallOverlay` surfaces it as a sonner toast — poor `error`, fair `warning`, good `success` — reusing the existing `call.networkQuality*` i18n keys. Unit: `callNetwork.test.ts` 15 tests incl. 5 quality paths; full: vitest 4183/4183, e2e 168/168.
- **Calls: preflight device check (Проверка устройств)** (`src/lib/call/CallManager.ts`): `startCall`/`acceptCall` now run `assertDevicesAvailable` before `getUserMedia` — a missing `navigator.mediaDevices`, or an `enumerateDevices()` list lacking the required audio/video input, emits `call:error` (`unknown` / `no-device`) and rejects, so a missing mic/camera surfaces before the peer is rung (reusing the existing `call.noDevice` / `call.startFailed` toasts); an empty enumeration (permission not yet granted) does not block. New public `checkDevices()` + `DeviceCheckResult` type. Unit: `callLifecycle.test.ts` 18 tests (mock extended with `enumerateDevices`); e2e: `e2e/calls.spec.ts` 10/10 incl. "accepting an incoming call fails fast when the required device is missing". Full: vitest 4178/4178, e2e 168/168.
- **Calls: network-error state on prolonged drop** (`src/lib/call/CallManager.ts`): a call demoted to `reconnecting` arms a one-shot 10s timer (`NETWORK_ERROR_TIMEOUT_MS`); if the transport is not restored before it fires, the call flips to `error` and emits `call:network-error` (recovery via `markConnected` works from both `reconnecting` and `error`). `CallStatus` gains `'error'`; `CallTopBar` renders the error label in the danger color via the `call.networkError` i18n key (all 8 locales). Unit: `callNetwork.test.ts` 10 tests incl. 3 network-error paths; e2e: `e2e/calls.spec.ts` "active call surfaces network error on a prolonged drop, recovery restores connected" (9/9).
- **Calls: error handling + lifecycle test coverage**: `CallManager.acquireLocalStream` maps `DOMException.name` to a `CallErrorReason` (`permission` / `no-device` / `device-busy` / `unknown`) and emits `call:error` before rethrowing; `answerIncoming` keeps the incoming sheet open when answering fails; `CallOverlay` shows localized error toasts and catches accept-handler failures. 4 new `call.*` i18n keys (`permissionDenied`, `noDevice`, `deviceBusy`, `startFailed`) in all 8 locales. New `src/lib/call/callLifecycle.test.ts` (12 tests: connecting→connected, no-connect-after-end, ended-log duration, incoming answer/reject/missed, missed-timer cancel, error-reason mappings, sheet-stays-open) and e2e coverage in `e2e/calls.spec.ts` (rejected call logged as declined; accept with denied microphone access shows an error and keeps the sheet open).
- **Settings feature-cycle e2e** (`e2e/settings-feature-cycle.spec.ts`, 10 tests): every `[role="switch"]` in every settings section must carry a non-empty accessible name and flip/revert cleanly; every cycle row (Font size, Relay Backend, Tor Bridge, Obfuscation Mode, Auto-wipe) must change value on click and restore; the Idle lock `<select>` changes and restores; TURN/proxy URL inputs accept text (proxy input behind the Use Proxy toggle); the Spam Filter state survives a reload. Every test traps `pageerror` and fails on any uncaught page error.
- **All-sites VPS migration kit** (`migrate/`): `export.sh` (read-only bundle: nginx, LE keys, PHP pools, systemd units, pm2, cron, web roots, SQLite data), `import.sh` (fresh Debian 12 restore), `migrate.ps1` (old→local→new orchestrator), `writers.sh` (cutover/rollback writer control), `INVENTORY.md` + `CUTOVER.md` (DNS flip, cert reissue, rollback). Prod dry-run verified: 385M bundle, all key files present.
- **Unit test suite for crypto/identity/state**: 13 test files (76 tests) covering `ed25519`, `safetyNumber`, `channelSigning`, `postKeyManager`, `masterKey`, `deviceKeys`, `devicePairing`, `secureStorage`, `retry`, `errorHandling`, and the `call`/`chat`/`settings` store slices. Crypto/identity tests run under the Node environment to avoid jsdom realm mismatches with tweetnacl.
- **`ed25519_sign` 1-argument overload** so the random-key signing path is type-safe.
- **Premium entitlement persistence refresh** (`src/hooks/usePremiumEntitlementRefresh.ts`, mounted in `App.tsx`): entitlement is re-fetched on foreground return (document `visibilitychange` → visible, `window` focus), throttled to 30s to absorb focus bursts after bootstrap; an expired entitlement is re-fetched immediately, bypassing the throttle.
- **Premium subscription client (Phase 1)**: `src/services/entitlements.ts` (device-key bound `GET /api/paymento/entitlement?pk=`), `premiumSlice` (memory-only entitlement state + startup refresh in `main.tsx`), and a new **Premium** settings section (plan rows, `sub:<devicePk>:<planId>` order IDs via `createPaymentRequest`, `PaymentRequestCard` with polling; success re-fetches the entitlement). Feature gating: call-recording retention (Forever requires Premium) and self-destruct timers (1 hour/1 day require Premium). `premium` i18n block (16 keys) added to all 8 locales; client/server plan parity guarded by `src/services/entitlements.test.ts`.
- **Premium feature gating (Phase 2)**: attachment size cap (free 50 MB → premium 500 MB) enforced in `useChatPreviewState` with a `premium.fileTooLarge` toast; extended reaction set (free 6 → premium 18) via `src/config/reactions.ts` gating the `MessageReactions` picker; ICQ sticker pack gated (`getIcqStickerIds` — first 24 free, full pack on Premium) in `StickerPicker` with a free-tier crown teaser button (`premium.stickerLocked` toast); new "What Premium unlocks" perks group in the Premium settings section. 6 new `premium.*` i18n keys (`fileTooLarge`, `perksTitle`, `perkFiles`, `perkReactions`, `perkStickers`, `stickerLocked`) in all 8 locales.

### Fixed
- **Chat: context-menu density, Shift+Enter newline, and Cyrillic Morse map** (2026-09-10): (1) `chat-preview/ChatContextMenu.tsx` desktop popup items were `min-h-11` (44px — mobile tap-target sized) with height estimate `items.length * 44 + 8` → too tall for a desktop context menu → items `min-h-9` (+ `py-1.5` → `py-1`), estimate `items.length * 36 + 8`; bottom-sheet variant kept at `min-h-11` (correct mobile tap target). Regression: `ChatContextMenu.test.tsx` height assertion `2 * 44` → `2 * 36`. (2) `chat-preview/ChatInputArea.tsx` Enter handler `onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && sendMessage()}` sent on every Enter incl. Shift+Enter (no newline input) → now `if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); sendMessage(); }` — Shift+Enter inserts a newline, plain Enter sends. (3) `src/components/MorseDecoder.tsx` Cyrillic `MORSE_MAP` block was almost entirely wrong (А, К, Ж, З, Й, Л, М, Н, О, П, Р, С, Т, У, Ф, Х, Ц, Ч, Ш, Щ, Ы, Ь all incorrect) → corrected to International Russian standard (Cyrillic shares Latin codes): А=.-, Б=-..., В=.--, Г=--., Д=-.., Е=., Ж=...-, З=--.., И=.., Й=.---, К=-.-, Л=.-.., М=--, Н=-., О=---, П=.--., Р=.-., С=..., Т=-, У=..-, Ф=..-., Х=...., Ц=-.-., Ч=---., Ш=----, Щ=--.--, Ъ=--.--., Ы=-.--, Ь=-..-, Э=..-.., Ю=..--, Я=.-.-; obsolete Ћ/Ќ entries removed. `decodeMorse` reverse map keeps first-code-wins (Latin over Cyrillic for shared codes) — accepted by design. Regression: new `src/components/MorseDecoder.test.ts` (8: Latin encode, Cyrillic encode, word separator `/`, unsupported chars, Latin round-trip, Cyrillic decode length, `isMorseCode`, `decodeIfMorse`). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5688/5688 (333 files).
- **CrmFilterBar: manage-departments button shrank to 16×44 in the side pane (§2.2)** (2026-09-09): ui-audit touch-target finding (company view, 1440×900) — the desktop filter row `hidden md:flex` holds 4 selects + 2 buttons inside the resizable side pane (240–480px, default 320px, `AppShell` grid); the icon-only Settings2 button had `w-11` but default `flex-shrink: 1`, so it collapsed to min-content 16px when the row overflowed the pane. Fix: `shrink-0` on the button (house pattern — assigned button already had it) + `flex-wrap` on the row so selects wrap instead of squeezing. Regression: `CrmFilterBar.test.tsx` +1 (button retains `min-h-11` + `w-11` + `shrink-0`; ui-audit e2e is the runtime guard). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5670/5670 (330 files), e2e 196/196.
- **ChatProfileView: Mute/Notifications row dedup, group member-count i18n, verified/bot badge polish, footer tap targets (§2.2/§1.1) + ContactProfileModal edit button touch fix** (2026-09-09): (1) `ChatProfileView.tsx` Notifications row duplicated the Mute row for group/channel (same `isChatMuted` state, same `setChatMuted(chat.id, !isChatMuted)` action — two switches toggling one state) and was dead local state for DM → row removed with `notifications` state + `notificationsOn` derivation; permission-switch indices shifted (`sendMessages` now `[1]`, was `[2]`). (2) Group subtitle `${memberCount} members` hardcoded EN → `t('profile.membersCount', { count })` ×8 locales, normalized to house `one`/`other` plural pattern (`ru.json` dropped `few`/`many`, `ja`/`ko`/`zh` gained `one`) under the `i18n.test.ts` locale-key consistency guard. New `storyShareLink(userId, storyId)` in `src/config/app.ts` (house convention, cf. `channelInviteLink`) emits `${APP_HOME_URL}#nexus://story/<uid>/<sid>` — opens the app home, mount handler opens the story viewer, `clearStoryDeepLink` strips the hash. `StoryViewer.tsx` `shareUrl` now uses it; dead constants removed (§1.3). - **StoryViewer: share button was a silent no-op on modern browsers (dead `navigator.share` path)** (2026-09-09): `src/components/stories/StoryViewer.tsx` `handleShare` preferred `navigator.share` when defined — modern desktop Chrome 128+/Edge/Safari define it (caniuse-verified), so the in-app share sheet (`StoryShareMenu`: forward to chat + copy link) never opened there; the payload was a bare custom-scheme `nexus://story/<uid>/<sid>` link (dead outside the app) and rejections were swallowed by `.catch(() => {})` → the button had no observable effect. Now `handleShare` always opens the in-app sheet (both actions live: forward via `forwardMessage`, copy link via clipboard); dead `navigator.share` branch + `shareData` removed. Regression: `StoryViewer.test.tsx` — "shares via navigator.share when available" replaced by "opens in-app share sheet even when navigator.share is available (never native share)" (defines mock `navigator.share`, clicks Share → sheet opens, mock not called); dead `delete navigator.share` setup removed from `beforeEach`. - **Transport/P2P: WebSocket heartbeat + fail-closed ECDH + connect timeout (2026-09-05)**: `server/signaling-server.ts` now sends authenticated-socket liveness pings every 30s and terminates sockets that stop responding; offer/answer forwarding forwards `dhPub`, `identityPub`, and `dhSig` (and no longer forwards the dead `hmacKey` field). `wsTunnel.ts` sends app-level `ping` every 30s, closes after 45s without `pong`, and swallows `pong` frames instead of dispatching them to app handlers. `P2PTransport.connect()` rejects with `Signaling connect timeout` after 10s, detaching the local `ws.onclose` before closing the socket to avoid a reconnect. Offer/answer handling is fail-closed when `dhPub` is missing or identity/HMAC authentication fails; failure paths clear `peerConnection`, `peerPublicKey`, `localDhPrivateKey`, `hmacKey`, `sessionAesKey`, and `pendingCandidates`, and the old plaintext HMAC fallback is removed. Regression: `wsTunnel.test.ts` (fake WebSocket/fetch/fake-timer heartbeat), `P2PTransport.test.ts` (connect timeout + no-`dhPub` offer/answer fail-closed state), `signallingPool.test.ts` (legacy `blocked` normalization). Gates: targeted Vitest 73/73 (5 files), ESLint clean, `tsc --noEmit` clean.

Regression: legacy `blocked` localStorage entries are normalized and remain selectable via `getNextAvailable()`. Gates: targeted Vitest 73/73 (5 files), ESLint clean, `tsc --noEmit` clean.

- **D5: dead "Enter App" after registration (empty handler)** (2026-09-04): `App.tsx` passed `onRegistrationComplete={() => {}}` into `AppAuthGate`, and `useIdentityAuth` computed identity status once on mount — after completing registration the status stayed `new-user`, so the "Enter App" button was a no-op and the user was stuck on the registration-complete screen (D5 empty handler). Fix: `src/hooks/useIdentityAuth.ts` now exposes `recheck` (`useCallback` wrapping the identity check, called by the mount effect); `AppAuthGate.tsx` removed the dead `onRegistrationComplete` prop and wires `RegistrationScreen onComplete={() => void recheck()}` (`LoginScreen onComplete` closes the login view); `App.tsx` renders bare `<AppAuthGate>`. Post-registration persistence verified: `RecoveryManager.generateRecoveryPhrase` → `storeMasterSeed` → `hasMasterIdentity()` true, so `recheck` flips status to `existing-user`. Regression: `useIdentityAuth.test.tsx` +1 (recheck re-runs the identity check and updates status), `AppAuthGate.test.tsx` re-targeted (`recheckMock` — asserts recheck called on registration completion, not called on login paths). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5550/5550 (324 files).

- **RecordingPlayer: jsdom `play()` undefined guard** (2026-09-04): `src/components/recordings/RecordingPlayer.tsx:29` — jsdom's `HTMLMediaElement.play()` returns `undefined` (no Promise), so the autoplay path's `.catch` threw a TypeError under the full suite. Guard: `const p = audioRef.current.play(); if (p) p.catch(...)` — a no-op in browsers where `play()` returns a Promise. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5549/5549 (324 files).
- **Deploy pipeline: PATH sanitizer in `scripts/deploy-all.ps1`** (2026-09-04): a literal `%PATH%` entry left in the Windows Machine PATH poisoned `cmd.exe` `%PATH%` expansion — `npm run lint` children could not resolve `node`/`eslint` (`'eslint' is not recognized as an internal or external command`, `deploy-all.ps1:85`). Machine PATH fixed (literal entry removed; backup `%TEMP%\opencode\MACHINE-PATH-backup-*.txt`), and the pipeline now sanitizes `$env:Path` before any npm call (strips the literal `%PATH%`, dedupes entries, warns when it changes) so stale/poisoned shells can no longer break the deploy. Full: lint 0, tsc clean (pipeline gates).

- **Connection status: hover legend instead of invisible native `title`** (2026-09-02): the status pill in the top-right corner (`TransportIndicator`) showed the current state (e.g. `Direct`) but hovering revealed nothing — it used the native `title` attribute, which does not render in the PWA / mobile webview context, so users had no idea which statuses exist or what they mean. `TransportIndicator.tsx` now renders its own React hover panel (`role="status"` + `role="tooltip"`): the **current** status (icon + label + meaning), plus an "All statuses" legend documenting every possible state — `⚡ Direct` (connected, direct P2P, no relay), `⟳ Connecting...`, `⚠ Degraded` (blocked/routed path), `○ Offline`, `✕ Error`. No native `title`; desktop hover and mobile tap (via `:hover`-style state + focusable) both show the panel. Regression: `TransportIndicator.test.tsx` +4 (legend on hover, hides on leave, all statuses documented, readable `aria-label`). Full: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 17/17 (TransportIndicator + AppChrome).
 (2026-09-02): the relay returned `500 {"error":"Token issuance failed"}` on every `/api/auth/token` request and the signaling WebSocket handshakes didn't connect. Live diagnosis (SSH + PM2 error log): `ReferenceError: crypto is not defined at handleToken (server/routes/auth.ts:70)` — `server/routes/auth.ts` called the **global** `crypto.randomUUID()` (lines 70, 124 for captcha session ids) without importing `node:crypto`, so on the server's Node runtime (no `globalThis.crypto`) token issuance threw and the bare `catch {}` folded it into a 500. Fix: `import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'` + both call sites now use `randomUUID()` (verified `server/routes/paymento.ts` already imports `node:crypto` for `randomBytes`, so it's unaffected). Also hardened: (1) `handleToken` catch now `console.error`s the real cause so future failures surface in PM2 logs instead of a bare 500; (2) `scripts/deploy-all.ps1` ensures `JWT_SECRET` is written to `.env` **before** the `pm2 restart`, so a relay never (re)starts into a missing-secret state. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean. The live relay must be re-deployed (`pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts/deploy-all.ps1 -SkipAdminCreate`) to receive the fixed `routes/auth.ts`; after that `curl -X POST https://mess.cvr.name/api/auth/token -d '{}'` returns 200 + `{token}`. Follow-up (same deploy): the server token endpoint then returned 200 live, but **stale service-worker cache** kept browsers on the old bundle (which seeded `wss://signaling1/2/3.messanger.app` for signaling). Root cause: `public/sw.js` Stale-While-Revalidate serves the cached `index.html` **cache-first**; because `CACHE_VERSION` never changed, the old `messanger-app-v8` cache persisted across deploys and the SW never reinstalled. Fix: bumped `public/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` v8→v9 (SW reinstalls + purges stale bundles) **and** `scripts/deploy-all.ps1` now auto-bumps `CACHE_VERSION` on every web deploy (reads `dist/sw.js`, increments `vN`, writes to both `dist/sw.js` and `public/sw.js`), so future deploys always bust the SW cache. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean. **Second follow-up (persistent `messanger.app` WS errors even after cache bump):** the browser kept dialing `wss://signaling1/2/3.messanger.app/ws` with a fresh bundle — root cause = **stale persisted seeds**, not the SW cache. `src/lib/network/signallingPool.ts` persists seeds to `localStorage['mess_signalling_pool']` and `load()`s them back on boot **before** registering the config's `initialSeeds`; a non-empty persisted set (old `messanger.app` hosts saved by an earlier bundle) permanently overrode the intended `wss://mess.cvr.name/ws` config and was never reconciled, so CACHE_VERSION bumps couldn't help. Fix: `SignallingPool` constructor now **reconciles persisted seeds against the current config** — drops any persisted seed absent from `initialSeeds`, re-adds any configured seed missing, and saves on change (config wins; stale seeds never survive a deploy that moves hosts). Regression: `signallingPool.test.ts` +2 (stale persisted seeds dropped in favor of current config; configured seeds restored after a stale run). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean.
- **Test flake: `paymento.test.ts` hook timeout under full-suite parallel load** (2026-09-01): `npx vitest run` failed `server/__tests__/paymento.test.ts` — `beforeAll` (2× real HTTP servers + SQLite admin seed + dynamic route import) exceeded the default 10s `hookTimeout` when the whole suite ran with `environment 1240s` / `setup 131s` of contention; the same file passes 17/17 in isolation (~2s). Root cause: vitest's default `hookTimeout: 10000` is tighter than the suite's own `testTimeout` budget in a saturated worker pool. `vitest.config.ts` now sets `hookTimeout: 30000` — hooks here are setup work (2 servers + 1 DB + 1 import), not long waits, so a 30s budget cannot mask a real defect. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5505/5505 (318 files).

Synthetic-mock lastSeen sites (`ChatPreviewLayer`/`ChatListView`) and the unrelated `OfflineBanner` sync-recency `60000` left as-is. - **Transport/messaging: hardening input parsing near untrusted boundaries (§3)** (2026-09-01): a security/resilience scan of raw network and third-party-input parsers found four unguarded `JSON.parse`/`window.open` sites that could throw in a WS/HTTP event handler or open an arbitrary scheme. `P2PTransport.ts` now wraps both signaling `JSON.parse` calls (`onmessage` connect handshake + `handleSignalingEvent`) in try/catch and ignores malformed frames (sibling `relayClient`/`relayRoster` already did); `services/paymento.ts` `postJson`/`verifyPayment` no longer throw on a non-JSON body (a 502 HTML error page would previously become an unhandled rejection on every payment call site) — parse-guarded with `{}` fallback, matching the earlier `createPaymentRequest` error path. `InlineKeyboard.tsx` url-buttons gained an http/https scheme allowlist before `window.open` (a malicious bot `javascript:`/`data:` url is now a no-op). Regression: `InlineKeyboard.test.tsx` +1 (blocks non-http(s) url buttons), `paymento.test.ts` +1 (non-JSON HTML 502 body → `Verify failed (502)`, no throw). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, focused vitest 3/3.

- **Workplace + storage: unhandled promise rejections on user-triggered actions** (§4.1): `WorkplaceView` fired four service calls from event handlers without `.catch()` — `tasks.createTask` (submit), `tasks.updateTask` (checkboxes), `automation.toggleRule`, `moderation.resolve`. The services layer can reject (`ServiceNotConfiguredError` and runtime `localServices` throws); the global `unhandledrejection` listener only logs, so a failure was silent to the user. All four now resolve through a new `reportServiceError(t)` helper that surfaces an error toast (`workplace.actionFailed` key, added to all 8 locales). `StorageSection.handleClearCache`'s `caches.keys().then(...)` CacheStorage chain had no `.catch()` (the surrounding sync `try/catch` cannot catch async rejection) → `.catch(() => {})`. `CrmView`'s `void ensureCrmSeed(...)` gained `.catch(() => {})` to absorb a corrupted-`localStorage` edge in `loadCrmPersisted`. Regression: new `WorkplaceView.test.tsx` (task-toggle rejection → error toast; create-task rejection → error toast). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, focused vitest 22/22.

- **Transport: stuck `connecting` after a deploy blip — dead-seed recovery in `SignallingPool`** (2026-09-01): a transient signalling outage (any `markFailed`, e.g. the PM2 restart during `deploy-all.ps1` dropping live WebSockets) persisted the seed's `failed` status to `localStorage` (`mess_signalling_pool`); `getNextAvailable()` filtered to `untested|active` only, so the sole seed (`wss://mess.cvr.name/ws`) was permanently excluded → `connect()` hit the null-url path → store parked at `blocked` and the app never reached `connected` again until localStorage was cleared. `SignallingPool.getNextAvailable()` now falls back to the earliest-`lastTested` `failed` seed when no `untested|active` candidate exists (returns `null` only for an empty pool), and `useAppConnection`'s window `online` handler calls `mgr.getPool().reset()` before reconnecting so a network return re-evaluates every seed. The reconnect loop thus keeps probing instead of parking. Regression: `signallingPool.test.ts` now 7 tests (the old "returns null when all servers failed" → retry-anyway; "null only when pool has no seeds"; "recovers across reloads after a transient failure"). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 5505/5505 (316 files).
- **Icon/font scale: off-ramp font sizes normalized** (2026-09-01): static `icon-font:audit` found 32 findings (10 F1, 22 F2). - **E2EE: DM channel now really end-to-end encrypted (per-session AES-GCM from X25519 ECDH)** (2026-08-30): the previous wire "encryption" was broken + asymmetric — a per-instance `TrafficObfuscator` singleton (`crypto.randomUUID()` key, never exchanged) encrypted on send but had no deobfuscation on receive, so payloads never round-tripped (and it was integrity-only, not confidentiality, contradicting the E2EE claim). `P2PTransport` now derives two independent 32-byte keys from the existing X25519 ECDH shared secret (`cryptoCore.deriveSharedSessionKeys`, SHA-512 digest split into HMAC + AES-GCM halves): outbound payloads are AES-GCM encrypted (`iv:cipher` base64), inbound are HMAC-verified then decrypted with the same locally-derived key, per-session ephemeral DH (+ Ed25519-signed keys, TOFU) gives forward secrecy + MITM resistance. Removed the broken singleton from the wire (`network.ts` no longer passes `trafficObfuscator`), dropped `setObfuscator`/`obfuscator` config; `obfuscationEnabled` now honestly means session encryption on/off. `TrafficObfuscator` kept intact for its own tests. Regression: `P2PTransport.test.ts` +3 (send→AES round-trip with same key, receive decrypts the same key, wrong-key payload dropped), ECDH test asserts 64-hex HMAC + non-null session keys, setObfuscator test → encryption-default. Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 4389/4389 (200 files).
- **Security: JWT algorithm pinned to HS256 (alg-confusion hardening)**: `server/auth.ts` admin sign/verify + relay-token sign set `algorithm: 'HS256'` / `algorithms: ['HS256']`, and `server/signaling-server.ts` `verifyWsToken` pins `{ algorithms: ['HS256'] }` — previously `jwt.verify` accepted the default algorithm set. Docs comment in `signRelayToken` corrected (confidentiality is client-side ECDH AES, not relay). Gates: lint 0, tsc clean, vitest 4389/4389.
